- Model identity, not a missed model: Di Cosimo 2015's only in-vivo system is the primary human NSCLC-derived xenograft LXFA629 (full text PMC5705197); the breast-cancer prediction rests on MCF7 in vitro. The track name 'Merck in-vitro and xenograft models' should say so, or the in-vivo arm be marked imported from NSCLC.

## breast-cancer-cdk4-6-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Does a negative adjuvant (early-disease) trial count against a model that only predicted antiproliferative sensitivity in advanced-disease cell lines? Under the brief it is either untested (a condition the model never tested) or contra (one drug's contra beats another's same, even though abemaciclib in monarchE and ribociclib in NATALEE succeeded). The context population ('HR-positive, HER2-negative') does not exclude early disease.
- model_kind has no in_vitro value; the draft uses ex_vivo for an established cell-line panel. Should in_vitro be added, or should ex_vivo be reserved for patient-derived material?
- Efficacy/dose parallel track rodent-brain-penetration-abema: does a rat intracranial glioblastoma survival model count as testing breast-cancer brain metastasis response, or is the breast brain-met trial a condition the model never tested (untested)?
- Safety track dog-cv-telemetry (palbociclib): when an FDA review duplicates text across a pre-FIH study and a later study run after patient QT data were public, which date governs retrodiction?

Missed comparisons:

- Efficacy: PALLAS (palbociclib + adjuvant endocrine therapy, HR+/HER2- early breast cancer) failed its primary iDFS endpoint. Pfizer press release 2020-05-29 (tier 5); Mayer et al. Lancet Oncol 2021, doi:10.1016/S1470-2045(20)30642-2 (PMID 33460574, epub 2021-01-15); NCT02513394 results 2022-04-13. Candidate contra for the cell-line model unless the adjuvant setting counts as a condition the model never tested (see rule question).
- Efficacy: PENELOPE-B (palbociclib, residual high-risk HR+/HER2- early breast cancer) negative for iDFS. Loibl et al. J Clin Oncol 2021, doi:10.1200/JCO.20.03639 (PMID 33793299); NCT01864746 results 2023-12-12.
- Safety: ribociclib dog telemetry predicted QTc prolongation at patient-level exposure, 'consistent with QTc prolongation observed clinically' (FDA 209092 Multidiscipline review, 2017; comparison stated). A same for the class that the draft omits; status unchanged, because abemaciclib's contra outranks it. Not checked: whether ribociclib animal studies were silent on its labelled hepatobiliary toxicity (a possible second contra).

## breast-cancer-esr1-degrader

Rule questions:

- Dose table has no row for 'exposure target reached and tolerated, drug abandoned before any efficacy readout' (GDC-0810, 2016; NCT02569801 halted by sponsor): is dose same on the exposure match alone, or untested until 'was it enough' is answered?
- When one component of a filed nonclinical package (hERG negative, single-dose dog telemetry clean) predicted no QT liability and another (repeat-dose dog ECG) predicted it, and patients showed it, is the package one track scored same with source_calls_it partial (count once), or does the contradicted component form its own contra track?
- Does a discovery-paper claim ('excellent in vitro and in vivo safety profiles') whose underlying rat/dog data are unpublished count as a stated model prediction for a safety same, and does an asymptomatic class AE (giredestrant bradycardia) absent from that claim count as silence contradicted on a harm the species could have shown?
- A member's Phase 3 failure known only from a press release (persevERA) on a track already contra: is a patient_trials row sufficient (one entry per change of status), or must it be its own tier-5 trial_unreported entry so the failure is visible?
- Dating a retrodiction (models check): decision 7 takes the earliest of registry results-posted, epub and print years, but for a retro entry the registry year precedes the model's first use (EpiIntestinal dose 12: DDI results posted 2024-08-16, model run 2025; Simcyp dose 10: DDI studies posted 2024, review 2026). Should registry dating be ignored for retro entries so the comparison never predates the model?
- PK/DDI prediction tracks in the dose cell (models check): the translational PBPK, Simcyp DDI and EpiIntestinal tracks predict the drug's own or a co-medication's exposure, not whether the exposure target was reached and sufficient (decision 4). Is dose the right cell, or do such tracks need a flag so they do not enter the dose track tally?

Missed comparisons:

- persevERA (NCT04546009): first-line giredestrant + palbociclib vs letrozole + palbociclib, 992 patients, 'did not meet its primary objective of a statistically significant improvement in progression-free survival' with 'a numerical improvement'; Roche ad hoc release, Basel 9 March 2026, https://www.roche.com/media/releases/med-cor-2026-03-09 (tier 5, trial_unreported). Belongs in efficacy patient_trials on er-pos-wt-breast-xenograft; the track is already contra so no status changes.
- Safety-pharmacology package as its own compared system: hERG negative to 30 uM (>1000x free Cmax) and a clean single-dose dog cardiovascular study at 400 mg/kg (FDA review NDA 219835 MultidisciplineR, lines 1882-1888) predicted no QT liability, yet patients showed mean dQTcF 10.9 ms (line 1064) and the label carries a 5.1 QTc warning. The draft pools this into the rat/dog repeat-dose track as same/partial; if the count-once rule does not pool it, it is a contra track.
- ZN-c5: Zentalis discontinued the oral SERD in August 2022 after phase 1/2 with no randomised readout (analyst coverage https://www.benzinga.com/analyst-ratings/analyst-color/22/08/28439032/this-analyst-says-zentalis-increased-focus-on-wee1-program-is-prudent; the Zentalis 10-Q was not fetched). No comparison is lost, but the draft's claim that ZN-c5 'never produced a randomised efficacy comparison' should cite the discontinuation.
- No patient-derived organoid or ex-vivo comparison with a member drug was found (web search); organoid-to-clinic concordance exists only for fulvestrant-containing regimens (e.g. PMC10979603, non-member), so no organoid track is missing.

## breast-cancer-hsp90aa1-hsp90ab1-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Dose: when the trial reports human PK but neither trial nor model source compares it with the model's effective exposure, and the trial itself suspects underdosing (Modi 2013), is dose 'contra derived_from efficacy' (drafter) or 'untested' (checker), and does efficacy stay 'contra' in that case?
- Efficacy, patient-level track: when the model's prediction is for a subgroup (TNBC) of a trial whose primary endpoint failed over the unselected population and the subgroup size is unreported, is the subgroup scored (contra), or does the track wait for a trial with that subgroup as its primary population? Can a tier-5 interim press release (ENCHANT-1 TNBC 2/11 PR) set a 'same'/partial on that track?
- Safety: when the model's stated prediction held (no cardiac or liver toxicity) but its silence was contradicted on another harm the species could show (diarrhoea), does the track net to 'contra', or does the held prediction stand as 'same' with the silence noted? The v3 safety definition asks both questions but does not say how they combine on one track.
- Efficacy: the trial was designed on the model (Grinshpun 2026 cites Friedland 2014) yet attributes its null to the population's defining condition (established endocrine resistance) that the model never represented. Does the brief's 'conditions the model never tested' pattern give 'untested', or does reliance on the model for exactly this question keep 'contra'?
- Dose, decision 16: is a toxicology package that states MTDs and a safety-factor phase 1 start, but names no effective exposure, a 'stated margin' (drafter: margin_stated true, same) or no margin (checker: untested)? Alvespimycin dose #4; the trial also never bridged its PK to any animal exposure.

Missed comparisons:

- Alvespimycin rat/dog toxicology, Glaze 2005 (10.1007/s00280-005-1000-9, PMID 15986212, epub 2005-06-29): prospective; predicted GI, marrow, hepatic (both species), renal and gallbladder (dog), stated MTDs and a phase 1 starting dose. Breast patients (Jhaveri 2012, 10.1158/1078-0432.CCR-11-3200): DLTs were grade III LV systolic dysfunction and reversible grade III keratitis, harms a dog study could show and the model was silent on; renal/gallbladder harms never appeared. Needs its own safety (and dose, margin_stated) track.
- ER-positive xenograft model of resistance emergence, Whitesell 2014 (10.1073/pnas.1421323111, PMID 25489079, epub 2014-12-08): sub-growth-inhibitory ganetespib had 'no overt anticancer activity on its own' but 'dramatically impaired the emergence of resistance to hormone antagonists' in mice. Grinshpun 2026 names it as the fulvestrant trial's direct preclinical rationale; the trial tested established resistance, so the emergence prediction is untested and the no-activity-alone statement is consistent with the null. Own track.
- ENCHANT-1 (NCT01677455) interim results, Synta press release 2013-12-12 (SEC exhibit 99.1: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1157601/000110465913090265/a13-26324_1ex99d1.htm): first-line ganetespib monotherapy, HER2+ cohort 3 of 4 evaluable objective responses (1 CR), TNBC cohort 2 of 11 PR (18%) and 5 SD; AEs diarrhoea, fatigue, nausea. Tier 5, trial_unreported; registry completed 2015-08 with no results. Belongs in patient_trials of the HER2 and TNBC tracks and bears on efficacy #4.
- Ganetespib first-in-human phase 1, Goldman 2013 (10.1186/1471-2407-13-152, PMID 23530663, epub 2013-03-25): plasma HSP70 elevated over a week post dose (engagement, downstream, mixed solid tumours), linear PK, MTD 216 mg/m2, RP2D 200 mg/m2, DLT grade 3 diarrhoea. A patient-side dose and engagement source for the ganetespib tracks the draft does not use.
- No patient-derived xenograft or organoid comparison with patient response was found for any member in breast cancer (WebSearch). Jensen 2008 ran patient-derived primary breast tumour clonogenic assays (MAXF lines) for luminespib GI50 but never compared them with patients, so no track is owed.

## breast-cancer-pan-her-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- The tile gives no population; the drafter set 'HER2-positive', which turns the sapitinib trials (MINT: no HER2 criterion in the registry, 'hormone-sensitive' in the abstract; THYME: HER2-low) into sibling-population comparisons that decision 12 transfers for safety only. Who fixes the population when the tile has none, and may one context carry a patient-level HER2-low prediction beside a HER2-positive class-level track?
- Does a single-arm phase 2 whose primary endpoint (PFS) has no prespecified bar count as 'the primary endpoint responded as predicted' (Park 2018, poziotinib, PFS 4.04 months, authors call it meaningful activity), so that it dates the track's first same? The draft excluded the phase 1 (Ma 2017, ORR 50 %) because its primary was MTD, which the rules support; the single-arm phase 2 case is undecided.
- Decision 16: does a model source published after the trial began but before the comparison was public (Li 2017, 2017-01-21; the pyrotinib phase 1 ran 2013-2016 and was published 2017-05-12) count as a margin 'stated before the trial'? The checker brief flags margin_stated with no pre-trial model statement; the drafter brief dates by what was public at the entry's date.
- When a model source asserts 'sufficient safety windows in animals' but its animal data are unreadable, is the assertion scored as silence (safety contra when the harm appears) or as a stated margin that held (dose same, safety same)? The draft scores it both ways at once (dose 3 margin_stated true; safety 3 silence contradicted).
- A patient-level prediction tested by the model only in other tissues (Hickinson 2010: HER2-non-overexpressing LoVo, FaDu, PC-9; the breast model was HER2-amplified) and paired with a breast HER2-low trial: is that 'a condition the model never tested' (untested) or a tested class-level prediction (contra)?
- Models track (er-pos-tamres-mcf7-xenograft, efficacy #6): a stated post-hoc comparison (decision 10 retro) whose patient arm is a sibling population of the context (MINT, HR+ HER2-non-amplified, in a HER2-positive tile). Does decision 12's safety-only transfer override decision 10, making it untested, as efficacy #3 was adjudicated, or does a patient-level track carry its own population so retro stands?

Missed comparisons:

- Morrison 2014, BCRT 10.1007/s10549-014-2878-x (epub 2014-02-20): ER+/HER2-negative MCF7 tamoxifen-resistant xenografts, AZD8931 + tamoxifen/fulvestrant delayed growth. This, not Hickinson 2010's panel, is the model behind MINT (AZD8931 + anastrozole, endocrine-naive). No track; paired with NCT01151215 results (2014-05-08, PFS HR 1.37/1.16) it is contra, prospective (published before the result).
- THYME, NCT00900627 (tier 3, results posted 2014-09-29): phase II PFS, AZD8931 + weekly paclitaxel vs paclitaxel in 'advanced breast cancer with low HER2 expression', HR 1.08 (95 % CI 0.76-1.52, p = 0.679); ORR OR 2.02 (p = 0.026). The direct HER2-low test of Hickinson 2010's pitch; the draft lists it with no result or quote. No peer-reviewed publication found on Europe PMC.
- Kim 2018, Cancer Res Treat 10.4143/crt.2017.303 (epub 2017-08-29): poziotinib phase 1, MTD 24 mg/day intermittent and 18 mg/day continuous, DLT grade 3 diarrhoea; 5 of 7 partial responses were HER2-amplified breast cancer. The poziotinib arm of track B has no dose entry and no PD readout; this is the source for both.
- Tjulandin 2014, Invest New Drugs 10.1007/s10637-013-9963-6 (epub 2013-04-16): AZD8931 phase 1 (NCT00637039), MTD 240 mg bid, DLTs grade 3 rash and grade 3-4 diarrhoea, exposure proportional to 160 mg. Track A's dose and safety cells cite no human PK or tolerability source.
- Hickinson 2010 full text (read): xenograft panel is BT474c (HER2-amplified breast), Calu-3, LoVo, FaDu, PC-9. The draft's track-A model description should say the breast model was HER2-amplified and the HER2-non-overexpressing models were non-breast.
- No patient-derived organoid or PDX comparison for any member in breast cancer was found (Europe PMC: pyrotinib + organoid/patient-derived + breast; the only PDX-style hit, 10.1002/ctm2.148, is gastric). No regulator document for pyrotinib is public (NMPA conditional approval 2018 on the Ma 2019 phase 2); nothing to add.

## breast-cancer-parp1-2-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- When a trial's own authors state the design cannot separate the class drug from a co-added chemotherapy (Rugo 2016: 'did not evaluate the individual contributions of veliparib and carboplatin'), is a met primary endpoint untested (prediction never isolated) or same with source_calls_it unresolved? Decision 4 states the 'conditions the model never tested' rule only for contra.
- Does an efficacy GEMM that reports 'no signs of toxicity' from weight, behaviour and necropsy alone count as askable silence on haematological toxicity (contra when patients show cytopenias), or must a scoreable safety prediction come from a study that measured the harm?
- A BRCA-deficient xenograft's chemopotentiation tested in a biomarker-unselected trial (BrighTNess, gBRCA stratified, roughly 15%) that fails overall with the genotype subgroup underpowered: contra on the model, as drafted, or untested under the 80% biomarker rule read in reverse? Confirmed contra here because Donawho's claim also covered BRCA-wt models.
- strengthens: true is defined (decision 7, #127) for a second miss that removes the first miss's excuse; the draft uses it for repeat matches (BROCADE3, pamiparib). Should repeat matches be entries or go to patient_trials?

Missed comparisons:

- PARPi-7 and BRCA1ness cell-line-derived expression signatures, pre-specified and tested against pCR in the I-SPY 2 veliparib/carboplatin arm (patient-level, in_silico): 'PARPi-7, BRCA1ness, and MP1/2 specifically associated with response in the VC arm but not the control arm.' Wolf 2017, NPJ Breast Cancer, https://doi.org/10.1038/s41523-017-0025-7 (PMC5572474). Same carboplatin confound as efficacy[2].
- 2X-121 (E7449)'s own BRCA-deficient xenografts: 'single agent had significant antitumor activity in BRCA-deficient xenografts' and 'the lack of toxicity'. McGonigle 2015, Oncotarget, https://doi.org/10.18632/oncotarget.5846 (PMC4747407). A member drug's own model with no track; opens untested 2015 (breast patient data unreported; Plummer 2020's two gBRCA breast patients progressed).
- Pamiparib's own preclinical package (Xiong 2020, J Med Chem, https://doi.org/10.1021/acs.jmedchem.0c01346: PARP1/2 IC50 1.3/0.9 nM, cellular PARylation IC50 0.2 nM, brain penetration; xenograft efficacy in full text, not checked). Paired with Xu 2023 it is a member drug's own model with no track; the draft scores pamiparib on the olaparib GEMM only.
- Kummar 2009's mouse PK/PD model (0.21 umol/L plasma target from single-dose PAR studies in mice) is stated in the source, not a pairing; same MX-1 track, so a field fix (comparison_stated true) rather than a new track.

## breast-cancer-pi3k-mtor-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Member-list completeness: gedatolisib is a dual PI3K/mTOR inhibitor with a positive phase 3 and a 2026 breast cancer approval but is not among the five members given; the drafter was told not to widen the class. Is the class table amended, and does the efficacy cell then carry a same track beside the voxtalisib contra?
- Dose 'reached' when the model source states no exposure, occupancy or regimen target: is MTD attainment enough to apply the 'reached, failed, abandoned' row (voxtalisib with letrozole), or is the dose cell untested?
- Decision 16 when the trial never states whether the MTD exposure met the preclinical effective exposure and attributes the dose-limiting toxicity to formulation rather than mechanism (dactolisib): is contra with blocked_by toxicity admissible, or must it stay untested?
- Safety silence: when the only public model source is an efficacy xenograft paper that says nothing about a known on-target class harm (hyperglycaemia) which the sponsor's unpublished tox package would have predicted, is the model's silence a miss (contra), or is the safety model the tox species and the cell empty/untested?
- model_first_use_year when the model characterisation paper post-dates first-in-human dosing (XL765: patients from 2007, Yu 2014): publication year or first documented use (e.g. AACR abstract)?
- Engagement from a dosed patient in another indication (lymphoma PD for a breast context): admissible as the draft assumes, and should imported_from be recorded on engagement entries as it is on safety?
- In-silico track identity (models check): Moein 2024's retrospective PK-PD-efficacy model was fit to a 786-O renal xenograft and phase 1 solid-tumour data, Salphati 2012's prospective model to an MCF7-neo/HER2 breast xenograft with allometric PK. Does a sponsor's later model of the same drug share the first model's model_id (gdc0980-genentech-translational-pkpd, as drafted), or is each fitted model its own in-silico track? Status is retro either way.

Missed comparisons:

- Gedatolisib (PF-05212384/PKI-587), a dual PI3K/mTOR inhibitor absent from the tile's member list, has a positive phase 3 in HR+/HER2-/PIK3CA-WT advanced breast cancer (VIKTORIA-1, NCT05501886; J Clin Oncol 2026, doi 10.1200/JCO-25-02643, PFS HR 0.24 triplet) and FDA approval 14 Jul 2026 (Drugs 2026 'Gedatolisib: First Approval', doi 10.1007/s40265-026-02394-y). Its model side is Mallon 2011 Clin Cancer Res (doi 10.1158/1078-0432.CCR-10-1993): MDA-MB-361 breast xenograft regression correlating with pAkt suppression. If the class table admits it, this adds a same track to engagement, dose and efficacy and a safety track.
- Patient-derived xenografts and patient-derived organoids of CDK4/6i- and ET-resistant ER+ breast cancer dosed with gedatolisib versus alpelisib (Sci Transl Med 2025, doi 10.1126/scitranslmed.adp5088): a patient-derived model system for the class with no track (retrodiction-era relative to VIKTORIA-1).
- Gedatolisib breast cancer cell-line/xenograft panel versus single-node PI3K/AKT/mTOR inhibitors (NPJ Breast Cancer 2024, doi 10.1038/s41523-024-00648-0) paired with the phase 1b gedatolisib + palbociclib + ET readouts (Lancet Oncol 2025, doi 10.1016/S1470-2045(25)00236-0; Clin Cancer Res 2025, doi 10.1158/1078-0432.CCR-25-0992).
- PTEN-deficient patient-derived xenografts dosed with PF-04691502 (Oncotarget 2017, doi 10.18632/oncotarget.19109) and LAR triple-negative breast PDX dosed with PI3K/mTOR inhibitors (Theranostics 2020, doi 10.7150/thno.36182): PDX systems used for the class with no patient comparison, i.e. untested tracks the draft does not open.
- Dactolisib randomized phase II versus everolimus in pancreatic NET (Oncologist 2018, doi 10.1634/theoncologist.2017-0144; 'not shown to demonstrate increased efficacy ... poorer tolerability') is the only randomized readout for dactolisib and could be imported for safety (stomatitis, hyperglycaemia) on the Novartis track.

## breast-cancer-pi3k-pan-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Decision 16: does a qualitative full-text tolerability claim ('very well tolerated doses', mice gained weight, 'data not shown') with no NOAEL/MTD number count as a stated margin? If yes, FERGI's toxicity-limited dosing is an independent window contra rather than a derived one, which changes the dose count but not the status string, so it never enters the status_draft != status_checked adjudication queue.
- Decision 4 (#35, #72) vs case 3: when the nominal dose reached the model's exposure target (340 mg >= 330 mg RP2D) but the trial paper attributes the primary-endpoint miss partly to toxicity-limited dose intensity (45% modifications, 34% discontinuations), is efficacy contra or untested?
- Decision 8: a genotype-annotated line panel from one paper (O'Brien 2010) scored as two model_ids by prediction_level. The rule puts prediction_level on the entry, so one track seems intended; confirm, since the split affects the headline computation.
- Dating a class-wide track's safety contra: does the first weak appearance of the harm on another member (pilaralisib grade >=3 AST 5%, 2015) set the year, or the decisive signal on the drug whose package is scored (BELLE-2 25%, 2017)?
- Decision 8: a PDX/organoid biobank that screens a member drug and claims translatability but never pairs with a named trial (Bruna 2016, Sachs 2018): is it a track opening at untested, or not a comparison at all?
- (models check) Decision 10 / decision 4: can a retrodiction be scored retro against a post-hoc, non-significant subgroup correlate (Nixon 2019: 3/3 clinical-benefit patients luminal A among 14 PAM50-typed, p = 0.07 one-tailed only when pooled with alpelisib, a non-member) from a phase Ib whose primary endpoint was MTD and which pre-specified no PoC gate? The brief's 'non-significant trend ... is not a patient result' is written for terminated trials; read broadly it makes efficacy 8 untested, read narrowly retro.

Missed comparisons:

- Bruna 2016, Cell (doi 10.1016/j.cell.2016.08.041, PMC5037319, epub 2016-09-15): breast PDTX/PDTC biobank screened pictilisib ex vivo (sensitivity in PIK3CA-mutant 3/15, PTEN-loss 5/15, high-pAKT 4/15 models) and dosed buparlisib in PDTXs in vivo. A patient-derived track with a patient-level prediction and no entry; pair with FERGI/PEGGY/BELLE-2 PIK3CA results (untested, or retro/contra depending on dating against FERGI 2016-05).
- Gao 2015, Nat Med (doi 10.1038/nm.3954, PMID 26479923): Novartis ~1,000-PDX 'PDX clinical trial' built to predict clinical trial response, 62 treatments across six indications including breast; buparlisib is a Novartis compound of that era. Full text not open access, so the BKM120/breast comparison is unverified; a member drug's own PDX model with a stated translatability claim and no track.
- Sachs 2018, Cell (doi 10.1016/j.cell.2017.11.010, PMID 29224780): breast organoid biobank whose drug screens were 'consistent with in vivo xeno-transplantations and patient response'; whether pictilisib or another pan-PI3K member was in the panel could not be verified (abstract only).
- Same-track model-side gaps, not new tracks: (a) the Genentech MDA-MB-361 breast-xenograft PK-PD model cited as ref 9 of Sarker 2015, which set the 6 uM.h target AUC for >=90% TGI, is the real dose-target source rather than Raynaud's exposure sentence; (b) ref 17 of Baselga 2017 (buparlisib plus fulvestrant regressed ER+ breast xenografts) is the buparlisib model side for the BELLE-2 comparison, which the draft scores only via Sanchez 2011 in vitro.
- (models check) Sachs 2018 Cell (doi 10.1016/j.cell.2017.11.010, epub 2017-12-07), full text read via cell.com PDF (PII S0092-8674(17)31319-3; earlier attempts used a wrong PII): pictilisib was in the organoid drug panel ('Pictilisib PI3K'; dose-response curves of afatinib and pictilisib for lines 10T and 27T, Fig. 7A), but in-vivo validation was afatinib only and the patient comparison tamoxifen only ('Tamoxifen was the only drug that resulted in differential responses'). A breast-organoid track opening untested in 2017 for pictilisib; no pan-PI3K comparison with patients in the paper.

## breast-cancer-pik3ca-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- 1. Does a randomised phase-2 neoadjuvant failure (NEO-ORB: ORR/pCR at 24 weeks, early-stage HR+ disease, letrozole backbone, PIK3CA-mutant cohort) make the xenograft track contra under decision 4, and with sticky contra (decision 6) and registry dating (decision 7: 2018-09-14 precedes SOLAR-1) does the track then stay red through three positive metastatic phase 3s? Or is neoadjuvant/early-stage disease on an endocrine backbone a 'condition the model never tested' (#35/#72), making NEO-ORB untested? Decision 12 says population = prescription population and enrolment goes in the note, which argues for the former. The same question decides whether the ER-feedback track (model: fulvestrant/tamoxifen) is touched by a letrozole-backbone failure.
- 2. Decision 16 literal reading: alpelisib's effective dose (300 mg) was reached and worked, with MTD 400 mg above it, but the predicted on-target toxicity occurred at the effective exposure (36.6% grade 3-4 hyperglycaemia, 25% discontinuations). Is dose same (row 'reached, drug worked'; the window existed) or contra with blocked_by toxicity ('toxicity appeared at or below the effective exposure')?
- 3. Pooled on-target safety track with member-level silence: alpelisib's rat/dog package predicted oral-mucosal lesions (stomatitis 24.6% in SOLAR-1, same), but inavolisib's package lists no oral target organ while INAVO120 had ~48-51% stomatitis. Does the earlier member's prediction carry on the pooled track (decision 12), or is the later member's silence contradicted and contra (decision 6: one drug's contra overrides)?
- 4. strengthens: true is used on an untested -> untested entry that adds a second prediction_level (patient) to a track; decision 7 defines it for a second miss. Is a second prediction level on one track its own opener entry, or a note on the existing one?
- 5. A sponsor toxicology track whose first public source is the 2019 FDA review: may the untested opener at model_first_use_year (2010) be omitted, as drafted, or must a proxy opener be written with a confidence note?
- 6. (models check, Vasan 2019 double-mutant track, efficacy 11) Decision 10 exempts 'a non-significant trend in a terminated trial' from being a patient result. Is a non-significant trend in a completed small phase 1 (NCT01870505, n=51) that explicitly motivated the model a patient result, and is a post-hoc subgroup split of an already-completed phase 3 (SANDPIPER, results 2019-01-25) a prospective test of the model or part of the retrodiction? Checker proposes retro; drafter scored same.

Missed comparisons:

- NEO-ORB, NCT01923168 (registry results 2018-09-14; Mayer et al. Clin Cancer Res 2019, doi 10.1158/1078-0432.CCR-18-3160): randomised phase 2, HR+/HER2- early breast cancer, PIK3CA-mutant cohort, alpelisib 300 mg + letrozole vs placebo + letrozole; primary ORR not met (43% vs 45%), pCR low; also alpelisib's own pAKT decrease in PIK3CA-mutant patient tumours (engagement patient_trials). The only member-drug failure in the context population and absent from the draft.
- LORELEI, NCT02273973 (registry results 2018-05-21; Saura et al. Lancet Oncol 2019, doi 10.1016/S1470-2045(19)30334-1): taselisib + letrozole neoadjuvant; co-primary ORR met (PIK3CA-mutant subset 56% vs 38%), co-primary pCR not met; a same + source_calls_it partial patient_trials line (decision 11) for the cell-panel track.
- Patient-derived xenograft of the index patient's PTEN-null metastasis (Juric et al. Nature 2015, doi 10.1038/nature13948, epub 2014-11-17): a patient-derived model built after the patient progressed that reproduced resistance to BYL719, i.e. its own track (model_kind animal, prediction_level patient, retrodiction true, status retro) rather than a patient_trials line under the cell-panel track (decisions 8, 10).
- Double-PIK3CA-mutant engineered cell/xenograft model (Vasan et al. Science 2019, doi 10.1126/science.aaw9032): abstract states double mutations predict increased sensitivity to PI3Kα inhibitors; the patient side (response in PI3Kα-inhibitor-treated patients) is in the full text, which I could not open through Europe PMC; candidate patient-level track, stated comparison.
- PIK3CA-mutant metastatic patient-derived organoids treated with alpelisib (Mol Cancer 2022, doi 10.1186/s12943-022-01617-6, PMC9306102): PDOs from mutant lesions responded, wild-type did not; no comparison with the patient's own clinical course found in the abstract, so at most an untested/retro PDO track (model_kind ex_vivo).
- Insulin-feedback mouse model (Hopkins et al. Nature 2018, doi 10.1038/s41586-018-0343-4): predicts that PI3K-inhibitor-induced insulin feedback reactivates tumour PI3K signalling and that diet/SGLT2 blockade widens the window; no patient comparison found, so an untested track if listed.
- EPIK-B5, NCT05038735 (alpelisib + fulvestrant after CDK4/6 inhibitor, PFS 7.4 vs 2.8 months, HR 0.52, trade-press reports only; no registry results posted): a positive patient_trials fact for the cell-panel track once a tier 1-3 record exists.
- INAVO121, NCT05646862 (inavolisib vs alpelisib, both + fulvestrant): the head-to-head that would test the Song degrader track; no results posted as of 2026-11-05, so the track's untested stands, but the NCT should be named in its note.

## chronic-hepatitis-b-virus-infection-hbsag-release-nap-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Opener dating (rule 7, #112): may model_first_use_year be a proxy earlier than the model's first admissible publication when tier-1/3 sources show the class entered patients on that model (REP 101 start 2009-01 on duck work published 2013/2015)?
- Retrodiction on the entry model (decision 10): the duck therapeutic prediction (Noordeen 2015) was published after REP 101 completed (2012-12) and after REP 102; the duck was the model the class entered on, but its admissible record post-dates the patient result. Prospective same (as drafted) or retro?
- A second prospective match on the same track outside efficacy (REP 2165 ~ REP 2139 in ducks, confirmed in REP 401): 'strengthens' (#127) covers a second miss only and patient_trials is efficacy-only. Where does a second match go?
- Decision 10 vs #73: a post-hoc model the class never relied on (woodchuck, HBV-transgenic mouse, SCID-Hu) that fails to reproduce the human result: contra under decision 10, or not scorable because the class never relied on it (#73)?
- Model identity (rule 8): is a re-engineered protocol of the same species/virus system by another lab (Debing 2024: s.c. dosing, experimentally infected adult ducks) a new model_id, or the dhbv-pekin-duck track? If the same track, a retro entry after a prospective same reads as a downgrade on the strip.
- Decision 16 anchor: is the 'effective exposure' the model's (duck 10 mg/kg/day i.p., not shown tolerated in cynomolgus at that weekly load; 27 mg/kg altered BUN/creatinine/albumin) or the clinical dose (250-500 mg weekly, inside the 9 mg/kg margin)? The draft's dose same rests on the clinical anchor.
- Class boundary (decision 12): Aligos' STOPS ALG-010133 shares the HBsAg-secretion mechanism and failed Phase 1 at the projected efficacious dose, but is not a tile member. Does the register treat STOPS as this mechanism, and if not, is a same-mechanism non-member failure recorded anywhere?
- Schema: model_kind has no in-vitro value; hepatoma-line assays (HepG2.2.15, HepaRG) are entered as ex_vivo.
- Safety (decision 4): a host-immune transaminase flare tied to antigen clearance in infected patients (95% of REP 401 NAP recipients, Bazinet 2021) that an uninfected toxicology species cannot show: is it 'a harm the species could have shown', or is same + partial right?
- Safety/dose: Roehl 2017's own calcium statement against the Bangladeshi symptoms is a stated retrodiction (monkeys did not reproduce REP 2055 hypocalcaemia, 2016). Contra under decision 10, or untested under #35/#72 because the sponsor attributes the symptoms to endemic heavy-metal load and a different member/salt?
- [models check] Safety retrodiction on an uninfected human-cell track (Real 2017, safety 5): the source states both a biomarker-level mismatch (serum cytokines rose in all REP 102 patients at therapeutic doses; cells secreted only at 50 uM) and a harm-level match (no pro-inflammatory clinical effects). Which level does decision 10's 'reproduces the human data' test: retro + partial as drafted, or contra?
- [models check] Magnitude bin for a model null against a multilog patient effect (Guillot 2017, engagement 10: no post-inoculation HBsAg change vs 2-7 log10 in REP 101/102): off_by_>5x as drafted, or the null bin? Decision 11 does not say which applies to a failed retrodiction.

Missed comparisons:

- ALG-010133 (Aligos STOPS, LNA-modified phosphorothioate oligonucleotide polymer, same HBsAg-secretion mechanism): Phase 1 ALG-010133-101 (NCT04485663, terminated) showed 'insufficient activity against hepatitis B virus' at the projected efficacious dose (400 mg, liver exposure >3x EC90), Aligos press release 2022-01-06 (tier 5, https://investor.aligos.com/news-releases/news-release-details/aligos-halting-further-development-stopstm-drug-candidate-alg), after prospective in-vitro HBV cell-model predictions (Aligos AASLD 2020 poster, https://www.aligos.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/ALG-010133-S-Antigen-Transport-inhibiting-Oligonucleotide-Polymer-STOPS-effectively-inhibits-HBsAg-secretion-in-multiple-Hepatitis-B-Virus-HBV-cell-models.pdf). Not a listed member (class_programs 3), so unscorable under decision 12 unless the register places STOPS in this mechanism; flagged as rule question 7.
- HepaRG / primary human hepatocyte entry assay for REP 2055 and REP 2139 (Guillot 2017, doi 10.1371/journal.pone.0179697, PMC5479567): a human-cell model used for the class with no track in the draft; likely an untested track (no patient comparison seen; abstract not read within budget).
- DHBV-infected primary duck hepatocyte assay (Noordeen 2013 in vitro, doi 10.1128/AAC.01003-13), the in-vitro arm the class entered on alongside the in-vivo duck; no patient comparison, so an untested ex_vivo opener at most.
- Roehl 2017 itself states a monkey-vs-patient calcium comparison (PMC5466589 discussion: 'showed no changes in serum calcium at doses exceeding those used clinically', against the Bangladeshi mineral-deficiency symptoms of Al-Mahtab 2016); the draft records it as an untested pairing rather than a stated comparison.

## chronic-hepatitis-b-virus-infection-hbv-rna-antisense-sirna

Rule questions:

- Does a model's explanation of a non-member's failure mode (chimp integrant finding for ARC-520, Wooddell 2017) count as a prospective prediction for a member designed around it (JNJ-3989), when the trial source never attributes the member's result to that design? (engagement 4)
- When the retrodicted patient result belongs to a non-member (ARC-520), does the opener carry retrodiction: true even though the status is untested either way? (engagement 2)
- Dose 'reached, drug worked -> same': must a model source state the exposure or regimen target, or does a drafter-made mg/kg mapping from a PK-only mouse paper suffice? (dose 2)
- When a member's later trial has a primary endpoint the model cannot express (REEF-2 off-treatment functional cure, 0/130), is that contra, same-partial, or no status entry? (efficacy)
- Do combination trials in which every arm includes a non-member class (MARCH: tobevibart + elebsiran) score for the member, and if so on whose primary endpoint?
- Are HBV-transgenic, hydrodynamic pHBV and AAV-HBV mice one track (hbv-genome-mouse) or three systems needing separate model_ids (decision 8)?
- Safety 3 (Gane 2023 PXB hALT1): the mouse run was motivated by the ALN-HBV volunteer ALT elevations already seen (2016) but predicted the member VIR-2218's volunteer result before it was known. Is a model that retrodicts the comparator and predicts the member 'same' (as drafted) or 'retro' under decision 10?
- Noack 2026 (engagement 6, 8): experiment dates are not stated and could predate the 2021 patient data. Does decision 10 turn on when the model result was run, or on when it was public (decision 7)? The drafter and this check used public date.

Missed comparisons:

- ARB-1467 (member) Phase 2 patient HBsAg data in Arbutus press releases, 2016-12-12 (mean max -1.0 log10 after three monthly 0.4 mg/kg doses, 3/5 >1 log; one discontinuation for transaminase elevation) and 2017 AASLD release: tier 5, admissible patient-side with model_src Wooddell 2013; the draft's note calling these 'congress abstracts only, not scored' is wrong. https://investor.arbutusbio.com/news-releases/news-release-details/arbutus-provides-additional-data-arb-1467-phase-ii-clinical
- NCT03672188 registry results record (elebsiran, posted 2021-12-13, tier 3): Maximum Reduction of Serum HBsAg From Baseline -1.03 to -1.65 log10 IU/mL across dose groups vs -0.07/-0.10 placebo; earliest tier<=3 member comparison, before Yuen 2022. https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT03672188
- REEF-2 (NCT04129554; Agarwal 2024 J Hepatol, doi 10.1016/j.jhep.2024.03.046, PMID 38583491, epub 2024-04-05; registry results 2024-07-31): primary endpoint functional cure met by 0/130 (HBsAg fell 1.89 log10 on treatment). A member's primary-endpoint miss the efficacy cell does not show.
- MARCH (Vir press release 2025-05-09, tier 5): study-defined primary endpoint HBsAg seroclearance 24 wk post-EOT 8% (4/51) tobevibart+elebsiran and 16% (5/32) with PEG-IFN in all participants; Phase 3 will not proceed without a partner. Every arm includes tobevibart (non-member mAb), so admissibility is a rule question. https://investors.vir.bio/news/news-details/2025/Vir-Biotechnology-Announces-Preliminary-24-Week-Post-End-of-Treatment-Data-for-Tobevibart-and-Elebsiran-Combinations-in-Chronic-Hepatitis-B-From-the-MARCH-Study/default.aspx
- Noack et al. 2026 Antimicrob Agents Chemother (doi 10.1128/aac.01127-25, PMID 41586499, epub 2026-01-26): elebsiran itself in AAV-HBV C57BL/6 mice (max 2.81 log HBsAg with muHBC34) and in HBV-infected human liver-chimeric mice (2.51 log), plus HBV-infected primary human hepatocytes (HBsAg EC50 1.4 nM). Published after the patient data, so a retro entry on hbv-genome-mouse with product_in_model true, and two untracked systems (human liver-chimeric mouse; PHH in vitro).
- NCT04667104 (PENGUIN) registry results posted 2024-07-03; draft records null.
- No tier 1-3 model source for ARB-1467's own preclinical data was found (Europe PMC returns reviews only); if none exists, its patient data pair with the shared mouse track via Wooddell 2013 (sibling-dosed).
- Rat repeat-dose hepatotoxicity (GalNAc-siRNA off-target) and NHP toxicology, Schlegel et al. 2022 Nucleic Acids Res (doi 10.1093/nar/gkac539, PMC9262600, epub 2022-07-01): ALN-HBV 'passed preclinical safety evaluations in both the rats and nonhuman primates' yet gave ALT >=3x ULN in 5/18 healthy volunteers (a silent-model miss on the non-member parent, product_in_model false), and the ESC+ redesign ALN-HBV02/VIR-2218 (member) showed an improved rat therapeutic window before re-entering the clinic. No rat or NHP tox track exists for this class. Also cites HepG2.2.15 RNA-seq off-target comparison of VIR-2218 vs ALN-HBV as the in-vitro basis.
- The Gane 2023 safety comparison (safety 3) used uninfected PXB mice as a human-hepatocyte hepatotoxicity model; the track id 'hbv-human-liver-chimeric-mouse' is labelled 'HBV-infected'. Same PXB chassis, so not split here, but the model name should read 'human liver-chimeric (PXB) mice' with infection status per entry (decision 8).

## chronic-obstructive-pulmonary-disease-adrb2-agonists

Rule questions:

- Rule 6 opening year: when the model paper postdates the first trial (AZD3199 trial start 2009 vs Stocks 2014; PF-00610355 trial 2008 vs Glossop 2010; vilanterol dog study FD2008 vs 2013 review), should model_first_use_year use the trial start as the 'proxy allowed', or the first public record as drafted?
- Does a pooled model-based (K-PD) analysis of a trial's primary-endpoint data (Nielsen 2012 for NCT00808288) count as the primary-endpoint result for the efficacy cell when the trial itself never reported?
- Does a medicinal-chemistry design claim resting on PK (rapid phase II clearance to minimise systemic effects, Glossop 2010) count as a model's stated safety prediction, or must the prediction come from a harm readout in a species?
- When the only patient-side safety comparison for a track is in another indication (AZD3199 potassium vs formoterol in asthma) and the context's own trial reports only 'no safety concerns', does imported_from permit same on the COPD track?
- For a human_genetic patient-level track (ADRB2 genotype and vilanterol response, Condreay 2016) where no genotype effect was found, is the status contra (the genotype model predicted differential response) or same (the sponsor's null hypothesis held)? The rules do not define the prediction of a pharmacogenetic analysis.

Missed comparisons:

- Condreay et al. 2016, Respir Med, doi:10.1016/j.rmed.2016.03.019 (PMID 27109822): ADRB2 Arg16Gly/Gln27Glu/Thr164Ile pharmacogenetics of vilanterol 25 ug trough-FEV1 response in 17 COPD trials (N=6075, 1795 on VI): no association. A human_genetic, prediction_level patient track for a member drug with no track in the draft.
- Boger & Friden 2018, CPT Pharmacometrics Syst Pharmacol, doi:10.1002/psp4.12270 (PMC5869554, online 2017-12-27): translational lung-PK model for 12 inhaled drugs including AZD3199 (and the PF-00610355 FEV1 data of Nielsen 2012), predicting human lung concentrations that correlate with published COPD trough FEV1. An in_silico retrodiction (retro) track with no entry.
- Aparici et al. 2016, Eur J Pharmacol, doi:10.1016/j.ejphar.2015.11.053 (PMID 26656755): dog bronchoprotection-versus-heart-rate safety index for vilanterol 2.4 (abediterol 10.5, olodaterol 4.9) and guinea-pig bronchoprotective t1/2 18 h for vilanterol, run after vilanterol's approval. A member drug's own model, retrodiction on the dog track; not used by the draft.
- Bjermer et al. 2013, Ther Adv Respir Dis, doi:10.1177/1753465813497527 (PMID 23907810; NCT00736489 results posted 2014-04-03): AZD3199 systemic effects (potassium, heart rate, QTc) versus formoterol in asthma; the patient side of the Stocks 2014 guinea-pig potassium comparison (safety 6).
- Program stops the draft calls unexplained: abediterol discontinued at Phase II in AstraZeneca's February 2021 pipeline (AdisInsight 800026746) and NCT04199598 terminated 2020 'business prioritizations ... not due to safety concerns' (tier 3 registry record); PF-00610355 stopped 2011 for strategic reasons (Wikipedia only, no admissible source found); AZD3199: no source found. None is a model failure, but the registry termination reason should be cited.
- Hughes et al. 2015, Drug Des Devel Ther, doi:10.2147/dddt.s66049 (PMID 25709399): AZD3199 systemic availability lower in COPD than in healthy volunteers or asthma; supports the dose-7 exposure note (optional).

## chronic-obstructive-pulmonary-disease-chrm3-antagonists

Rule questions:

- Rule 6 dating of a paired (comparison_stated false) entry: once the model paper is public, does a tier-3 registry results posting that carries the primary endpoint but no model comparison set the year? Drafter dated to the first paper (AZD9164 2012 not 2011; revefenacin 2018 not 2016); rule 6's text lists registry posting first.
- Safety: a model's stated comparative prediction (Pulido-Rios 2013: 'improved tolerability profile compared with marketed muscarinic receptor antagonists') held against placebo but not against tiotropium over 52 weeks (5.4-7.7% vs 6.1% antimuscarinic AEs). v3 defines contra/opposite for a predicted harm that never appeared, not for a predicted reduction of harm that failed. same + partial, or contra?
- Member drug AZD9164 is, by its sponsor's own description (Jorup 2014), equipotent at human M1, M2, M4 and M5. The tile lists it as a CHRM3-antagonist member so it was scored; does a listed member that is in fact non-selective stay inside the class, or does the 'non-selective drug outside the member list' exclusion extend to it?
- Dose entry 2 (AZD9164): the guinea-pig lung-vs-salivation therapeutic index is a stated margin that held in patients (systemic effects only at 3x the effective dose) while the program died on an unpredicted harm scored in safety. Decision 16 seems to give margin_stated true here; confirm that a held margin is recorded even when the track's safety cell is contra on another harm.
- Safety from a tier-3 registry AE table (NCT01033487: no antimuscarinic AE on PF-03635659, single dose, n=22) with no quotable sentence: does rule 3 keep it untested, or can an AE-table row serve as the quote the way an outcome-measure title does for efficacy?
- Retrodiction boundary when the model's run date is undocumented: revefenacin human bronchi are first evidenced in the IND 119840 review (2014-02-21), after the sponsor's Phase 2a NCT01704404 (completed 2013-12) but before the paired NCT02040792; is 'prospective unless shown otherwise' the default?

Missed comparisons:

- PF-03635659 has a published model side the draft says does not exist: Glossop 2011 J Med Chem doi 10.1021/jm200884j (guinea-pig tracheal-strip off-rate plus in vivo duration-of-action models; 'in vivo duration of action studies confirming its potential for once-daily use in humans'). Paired with NCT01033487 (results posted 2016-02-19: trough FEV1 +51/+79/+93 mL vs placebo at 180/580/1450 ug, tiotropium +61 mL) it is a second track (Pfizer guinea-pig trachea/in vivo duration) scoring same on engagement, dose and efficacy in 2016.
- Human isolated bronchial tissue (ex vivo) for revefenacin has no track: Hegde 2018 Pharmacol Res Perspect doi 10.1002/prp2.400 (human bronchi, antagonism slowly reversible t1/2 >10 h) and the FDA pharmacology review ('human bronchial tissue (1 - 1000 nM TD-4208)'); label 12.1 says 'In preclinical in vitro as well as in vivo models ... The clinical relevance of these findings is unknown.' At least an untested track (model_kind ex_vivo), or paired with the 24-h trough FEV1 data.
- AZD8683 on the AstraZeneca guinea-pig track is named but not scored: Mete 2013 doi 10.1016/j.bmcl.2013.09.092 ('inhibition of bronchoconstriction and duration of action, as well as systemic side effects ... assessed in guinea-pig models') pairs with NCT01205269 (results 2013-11-13, primary peak and 22-26 h FEV1) and NCT01708057 (results 2013-09-30, vs tiotropium, terminated). Both a second drug's same on the track and the open safety question of whether AZD8683 reproduced the post-dose FEV1 fall (registry AE tables).
- Revefenacin patient-side sources that pre-date the 2018 FDA review and move the track's dates: NCT02040792 results 2016-12-07 (28-day, primary D28 trough FEV1 met), NCT02109172 results 2016-12-08, NCT01704404 results 2017-03-13, Quinn 2017 doi 10.1016/j.pupt.2017.10.003 (epub 2017-10-04; primary trough FEV1 day 7 met, 'no indication of significant systemic anti-muscarinic activity at any dose'), Pudi 2017 doi 10.1186/s12931-017-0647-1 (epub 2017-11-02).
- QAX028 and CHF5992: no model paper found on PubMed under either code (QAX028, QAX-028, CHF5992, CHF-5992); NCT01068613 has no posted results. The draft's unscored call for both stands; the search was PubMed only.
- Hegde 2018 (doi 10.1002/prp2.400) also reports rat isolated trachea (ACh antagonism, offset t1/2 13.3 h) and guinea-pig isolated trachea EFS (offset >16 h) ex-vivo models under the same 'consistent with emerging clinical data' sentence; neither has a track (the rat/dog track is in vivo). Minor: same paper and comparison, could be noted on the human-bronchus track rather than tracked.

## chronic-obstructive-pulmonary-disease-mapk14-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- A primary endpoint that is statistically positive in direction (PH-797804 12-week trough FEV1 +32 to +60 mL on tiotropium, 95% CIs excluding zero; +27 mL on ICS/LABA with C1 met) but below the sponsor's pre-specified decision bar (>=50 mL at >0.50 posterior probability) and below MCID, after which the drug was dropped: is that 'reached, failed, abandoned' -> contra, or 'same' with source_calls_it partial? Decision 13 says judge on data, but names no bar for a direction-only model. The answer decides efficacy entries 3 and 7 and dose entry 3.
- When the registered primary endpoint is a within-arm change from baseline with no placebo contrast (AETHER NCT02700919: declared met by the sponsor, p=0.012/<0.001 within arm; vs-placebo +27/+58 mL, p=0.51/0.15), does the atlas score the registered primary as met or the placebo-corrected effect as null?
- A model statement that systemic exposure will be sub-pharmacological (Pehrson 2018, AZD7624) is an exposure claim, not a named harm. Does it score in safety as the draft does, as a dose margin (margin_stated), or not at all?
- When a source states a comparison with one trial (Singh 2015 -> 6-week PH-797804 win) while a higher-tier record of a later trial of the same drug on the same track was already public at the entry date (12-week miss, 11 Jul 2015), does rule 7 require the entry's status to reflect the whole record as of that date, or does the stated comparison stand with the miss as a separate same-year entry?
- A sponsor-authored model paper (Growcott 2018, all-Novartis authors, received 2017-08-18) appeared after the sponsor's own trial completed (NCT01332097, 2013-05) but before any public patient record (Strambu 2019 epub 2019-11-04; registry 2023-03-21), and neither dates its experiments nor cites the trial result as motivation. Does decision 10 date a retrodiction by the sponsor's private knowledge (retro, as drafted) or by the public record (same), and who carries the burden when the run date is unknown? Decides safety entry 4 of bct197-mouse-influenza-infection.

Missed comparisons:

- Armstrong 2011, doi:10.1124/jpet.111.180737 (JPET 338:732, PMID 21610141): COPD-patient alveolar macrophages ex vivo; non-member sibling BIRB-796 synergises with dexamethasone on LPS-induced cytokines, predicting add-on benefit of a p38 inhibitor over a corticosteroid. Tested by PH-797804 6 mg on salmeterol/fluticasone background (NCT01321463; EudraCT 2010-024219-15 Public Disclosure Synopsis published 2016-06-02: 27 mL, C1 met at 0.927, C2a missed at 0.104). The draft's copd-ex-vivo-alveolar-macrophages track has no efficacy entry; this pairing gives it one (class level, product_in_model false, comparison_stated false, year 2016).
- Mereo BioPharma RNS 2017-12-11 'Positive results from Phase 2 trial with BCT-197' (https://www.lse.co.uk/rns/positive-results-from-phase-2-trial-with-bct-197-xivpowfecz8pva2.html; also mereobiopharma.com news): tier-5 source declaring AETHER's registered within-arm Day-7 FEV1 primary met (p=0.012 high dose, p<=0.001 low dose; placebo p=0.102) three years before the registry posting of 2020-11-30. The draft's note reads AETHER as a miss on the vs-placebo contrasts (+27/+58 mL, p 0.51/0.15, confirmed on NCT02700919) without showing this; it should be listed with trial_unreported handling and the rule question below.
- Yu 2022, doi:10.3389/fphar.2022.950035 (Front Pharmacol, PMC9554617): tier-4 meta-analysis of 10 p38-inhibitor COPD RCTs (n=1,751) finding no efficacy or safety difference versus placebo. It pools non-members (losmapimod) so it cannot set a member-only status, but it is a patient-side record the draft should cite with that caveat.
- Millan 2011, doi:10.1021/jm200677b (J Med Chem, PMID 21888439): PF-03715455 design paper states 'efficacy and safety profiles confirming its potential as an inhaled agent'. If it carries a rodent LPS result for this member, the rodent-lps-lung-neutrophilia track should record product_in_model true for PF-03715455. Abstract-level only; the model data were not verified.

## colorectal-cancer-anti-egfr-antibodies

Rule questions:

- Is a meeting abstract with a DOI (ASCO/ESMO supplement) a tier 1 source? The draft relies on one for GAIN-C (efficacy 8); if abstracts are inadmissible that contra has no other record, and if admissible, ASCO 2013 abstract 3551 re-dates four 2015 entries to 2013.
- Does a tier 5 press release (or a conference abstract) set `year` under rule 6 when the status rests on a later tier 1 paper of the same trial (Sym004 phase 2: Symphogen 2017-09 vs JAMA Oncol 2018)?
- Safety silence: Skartved 2011 measured clinical chemistry in cynomolgus and reported no magnesium finding, yet hypomagnesaemia was a human DLT. Is EGFR-dependent renal magnesium wasting a harm the species 'could have shown' (contra) or excluded like CRS in mouse?
- Track placement of a retrodiction on an existing platform: do the ECD-mutant cell lines and xenografts (Sanchez-Martin 2016) stay on sym004-egfr-resistant-xenograft with retrodiction true, or take their own model_id like the PDX panel?
- Is a single-patient 'proof-of-principle' (n=1) an admissible comparison for a patient-level `same`? The rules require >=1 drug, not a minimum number of patients.

Missed comparisons:

- Napolitano et al. 2017 Oncotarget (PMID 28978055): Sym004 in CRC xenografts with acquired cetuximab resistance and MET activation; a CRC-specific resistance model on the Sym004 platform (model_src for the 2018 contra; MET-amplified subgroup never tested in patients).
- Br J Cancer 2014 (PMID 24496456): imgatuzumab ADCC by NK cells taken ex vivo from colorectal cancer patients, irrespective of chemotherapy; a patient ex-vivo model (model_kind ex_vivo) with no track, never compared with outcome (untested).
- Dienstmann et al. ASCO 2013 abstract 3551, DOI 10.1200/jco.2013.31.15_suppl.3551 (online 2013-05-20): phase 1 expansion PD, activity and DLTs public two years before Cancer Discov 2015; sets 2013 dates if abstracts are admissible.
- Symphogen press release 2017-09 (symphogen.com/2017/09/sym004-randomized-phase-2-study-results/; tier 5) and ESMO 2017 abstract 478O (Ann Oncol 28 suppl 5): 'did not meet the primary end-point of a 3-month improvement of overall survival'; earliest public record of the phase 2 failure.
- Ann Oncol 2017 (PMID 28950289): randomized window study of imgatuzumab vs cetuximab pharmacodynamics in operable HNSCC; importable engagement comparison (imported_from HNSCC) for the imgatuzumab track.
- Patient PK sources with no entry: Japanese phase 1 (Cancer Sci 2018, PMID 30099818) and Sym004 population PK (J Pharmacokinet Pharmacodyn 2020, PMID 31679083); later patient PK is its own dated dose entry under the brief, status unchanged.

## colorectal-cancer-kdr-vegfr-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Active same-pathway comparator: BATON-CRC (tivozanib+mFOLFOX6 not superior to bevacizumab+mFOLFOX6 on PFS, 2016). The xenograft predicted activity vs vehicle; the trial tested superiority over a VEGF-pathway comparator. Is non-superiority to an active comparator of the same pathway scorable as efficacy contra, or is it 'conditions the model never tested' (untested)? The drafter chose not to score it; the v3 rules do not decide this explicitly.
- Safety track identity: do species-specific safety-pharmacology studies run for different member drugs (cynomolgus telemetry for tivozanib, beagle telemetry for fruquintinib) belong to one 'tox-rodent-nonrodent' track, so that fruquintinib's contra overrides tivozanib's same, or is each species/drug package its own system?
- Design-limited silence: a single-dose, 6-hour dog telemetry study cannot show hypertension that develops over days to weeks. The v3 safety rule keys only on whether the species could show the harm. Should a study design that could not show it count as contradicted silence (contra) or as untested?
- Engagement across indications: the tivozanib DCE-MRI/sVEGFR2 readout comes from a phase I in advanced solid tumours, not CRC. Since healthy volunteers count for engagement, any dosed humans presumably count; confirm that engagement entries from other-indication patients need no imported_from.
- Quote attribution inside patient_trials: when the quoted sentence comes from an FDA review rather than the DOI listed, should the entry carry the review URL as its source? Status-neutral, but lint does not catch it.

Missed comparisons:

- Human PD readout for tivozanib: Eskens et al. 2011, Clin Cancer Res, doi 10.1158/1078-0432.CCR-11-0411 (PMID 21976547) — 'Dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI indicated reduction in tumor perfusion'; plasma VEGF-A up and sVEGFR-2 down dose-dependently. Matches Nakamura 2006's rat DCE-MRI/pVEGFR2 prediction; the draft says it could not locate this. Engagement should carry a 2011 same entry (readout_tier downstream).
- EMA Fotivda EPAR, EMA/CHMP/437168/2017 (22 Jun 2017, https://www.ema.europa.eu/en/documents/assessment-report/fotivda-epar-public-assessment-report_en.pdf): regulator-stated safety-pharmacology-to-clinic blood-pressure comparison (model side rats/monkeys; 48% hypertension AEs in study 301), the tier-2 record four years before the FDA 2021 review; also the 14-model nude-rat xenograft panel (Study PRT/02149.1) with LoVo colon carcinoma regression at 1 mg/kg, and phase I DCE-MRI/sVEGFR2 text. Should replace the FDA 2021 review as src for safety entry 2 and be cited on the tivozanib xenograft track.
- Rivoceranib (apatinib) CRC xenografts, model side for the member drug the draft left unmodelled: LoVo colon xenograft vessel-normalisation study, Acta Pharmacol Sin 2019, doi 10.1038/s41401-018-0058-y (PMID 29977004); and Onco Targets Ther 2020, doi 10.2147/OTT.S266549 (PMID 33154652), apatinib CRC xenograft via VEGFR2/beta-catenin. Both sit on the crc-xenograft-rodent track; patient side is single-arm only (Oncologist 2019 doi 10.1634/theoncologist.2019-0164; Clin Colorectal Cancer 2018 pilot doi 10.1016/j.clcc.2018.02.011), so the rivoceranib arm stays untested, but the model should be listed, not described as 'no CRC-specific preclinical model found'.
- Sun 2014 abstract states fruquintinib was tested in 'a panel of tumor xenograft and patient derive[d] xenograft models in mouse'. If any PDX was colorectal, PDX is a second system with no track. Full text could not be retrieved (Europe PMC fullTextXML HTTP 500; NCBI efetch returned abstract only); unverified.
- Searched (Europe PMC, title/abstract) for patient-derived organoids or ex-vivo patient cells compared with response for fruquintinib/rivoceranib/tivozanib in CRC: none found. No randomized rivoceranib CRC trial found (only single-arm/pilot and meta-analyses); no press-release failure beyond BATON-CRC found. FRUQUITAS (TAS-102 +/- fruquintinib, phase III) is ongoing, design paper only.

## colorectal-cancer-vegfr-multikinase-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Dose: the model's exposure target was reached (by the drafter's arithmetic from published clearance and dose; no source states the ratio) but that drug's efficacy in the context is untested under #32/#80, while a sibling member with no exposure target won on PFS. Does decision 4 case 2 pool across members, or is the dose cell untested? The case table has no row for reached-with-efficacy-untested.
- Chan 2017 (single-arm, missed ORR primary, conclusion 'linifanib has some anti-tumor activity', 63.5% SD, PFS 4.7 months): does that conclusion count as the source 'calling it a match' under #32, making it same + source_calls_it partial (product_in_model true, datable 2014 by the registry record) rather than untested? The track is same either way; the carrying drug and year change.
- Population: does a biomarker-selected trial (KRAS-mutant only) count for an 'Unselected' context? The >=80% rule runs only from unselected trials to biomarker contexts.
- Decision 10 keying: the harm was known for the class (VEGFR-TKI hypertension, 2005-2006) and for linifanib only from phase-1 conference reports when the rat telemetry model was run. Does class-level knowledge suffice to make the model a retrodiction? I applied it, on the Franklin 2009 abstract's own framing.
- A primary ORR gate for a cytostatic class whose model predicted growth inhibition, not regression: #53 ('failed = the predicted effect was absent') makes the missed ORR a non-failure, but the efficacy rule's 'trial's primary endpoint' wording invites a contra. Worth stating explicitly.
- Dose cell, PBPK track: Yu 2014 reproduced human famitinib exposure within 2-fold but set no effective-exposure target, so sufficiency is undetermined. Does decision 10 give retro on the reached half alone, or does decision 4 (reached AND sufficient) make a target-less PK model untested, as dose index 2 was adjudicated? I confirmed retro as drafted.
- Openers of post-hoc models: lint (line 68) rejects retrodiction: true on any status but retro/contra, so the untested opener of a retrodiction model (engagement 3, dose 3, and safety 1) necessarily reads as prospective at first use; the safety index 1 verdict asked for the flag there, which lint would refuse. Decide whether openers carry the flag or the strip reads it from the next entry.

Missed comparisons:

- NCT01365910 registry results record (tier 3, posted 2014-08-26, https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT01365910): hypertension in 20/30 linifanib CRC patients and ORR 0/30 against a >=15% target. The draft says the record does not itemise hypertension; it does. It dates the CRC-only safety comparison to 2014 and gives the efficacy patient_trials entry a quotable primary result.
- No untracked model found. Europe PMC sweeps for (famitinib OR SHR1020), donafenib and (linifanib OR ABT-869) with colorectal/colon x xenograft/organoid/patient-derived/PDX terms return no CRC preclinical paper for any member (donafenib hits are all HCC); WebSearch likewise. Famitinib's and donafenib's own CRC models, if any, are unpublished, as the draft says.
- FACT (NCT02390947, completed 2019-07, last update 2020-12-22) and the donafenib phase 3 (NCT02870582, completed 2020-04-30, last update 2022-11-29): no posted results, publication or press release found by WebSearch; the draft's 'unreported' stands. Famitinib + camrelizumab CRC basket (Innovation 2025, PMID 39872476) is a PD-1 combination: patient_trials at most, no status.
- Models check (CHECKER_BRIEF_v3 / models): Europe PMC (ABT-869 OR linifanib) AND (DCE-MRI OR Ktrans), 73 hits, finds no untracked CRC model. Luo 2012 (PMID 22080168) is an orthotopic rat glioma DCE-MRI model, out of tile; Koh 2011 (PMID 21432928, same phase 1, PS fell >25.1% in late progressors) is patient-side and changes no status.

## crohn-disease-anti-il23a-antibodies

Rule questions:

- Decision 16: a stated NOAEL margin held (patients dosed to 600 mg IV, dose approved, never lowered) but an unpredicted labelled harm (hepatotoxicity, W&P 5.4) appeared at that exposure. Is 'toxicity appeared at or below the effective exposure' met (dose contra, blocked_by toxicity), or does 'the harm that closes the window' limit contra to harms that actually blocked the dose (dose same, harm counted once in safety, as drafted)? The text supports both readings; I accept the draft's.
- Decision 16 / checker brief: must the margin be stated against this context's regimen before the trial, or does a pre-trial NOAEL (public 2019 in the psoriasis review, studies older) whose ratio to the Crohn's dose was computed by the regulator in 2022 qualify as margin_stated: true?
- Decision 8 granularity: are the T-cell-transfer (adaptive) and anti-CD40 (innate) SCID colitis models, both dosed with the same surrogate PR-963484, one system (one model_id, as drafted) or two tracks? Likewise IL-23p19-knockout colitis (Yen 2006, doi 10.1172/JCI21404), which the drafter left unopened because nobody compared it with patients.
- Patient-level predictions that are not model systems: brazikumab's baseline serum IL-22 responder biomarker (Sands 2017) is a prediction_level patient claim with no model_kind in the enum, and INTREPID's termination left it uncompared. Should biomarker-stratification predictions have a track at all?
- Member list: guselkumab (anti-IL-23p19, to my knowledge FDA-approved for Crohn's in 2025, not verified here) is absent from the given members (brazikumab, mirikizumab, risankizumab). The drafter correctly did not widen; the class table may be missing a fourth approved member.
- [models] Decision 10: after a retrodiction, does a later confirmatory trial of the same claim in the same population (MOTIVATE phase 3 after the retrodicted 2017 phase 2; trial registered 2017-04-07, before the model) count as the model's 'first prospective prediction afterwards' (same), or must the prediction be one the clinic had not already shown (track stays retro)? The efficacy[7] correction rests on the second reading.
- [models] Decision 4, in-silico dose tracks: the sponsor popPK model (Suleiman 2019) predicted the phase 3 regimen's exposures, but 'sufficient' came from observed phase 2 dose-response and a post-hoc ER fitted to phase 3 data (761262 s14.4.2). Does 'reached, drug worked' apply to the exposure prediction alone, or is the ER half untested with source_calls_it: scaling?

Missed comparisons:

- No model system with a patient comparison was found beyond the three tracks. Europe PMC and web sweeps for anti-IL-23p19 patient-derived organoid, explant or ex-vivo tissue, humanized-mouse, and IL23R-genotype-predicts-response work for brazikumab, mirikizumab or risankizumab returned only reviews; IL-23 acts on immune cells, so no epithelial organoid track is expected.
- Not a new system, a better model_src: Steere 2023 J Pharmacol Exp Ther 'Generation and Characterization of Mirikizumab' (doi 10.1124/jpet.122.001512) is the published tier-1 home of the LSN2479016 surrogate colitis data the draft cites only through the 761279 UC review judgement; title seen, not opened.
- Set aside under decision 13, not a track: IL23R pharmacogenetic prediction of ustekinumab (anti-p40, not a member) effectiveness, e.g. doi 10.3390/ph19091428 (2026 review); no p19-member equivalent exists.
- Earlier public source for the brazikumab termination: AstraZeneca press release 1 June 2023 (https://www.astrazeneca.com/media-centre/press-releases/2023/update-on-brazikumab-development-programme.html), tier 5: timeline delays and competitive landscape, 'No safety concerns were identified for patients in these trials'. The draft dates the stop to the 2026-06-23 registry posting; changes no status.

## cystic-fibrosis-cftr-correctors

Rule questions:

- Decision 16 / checker brief: does a tox margin first published in the NDA review (after Phase 3) count as 'stated before the trial' when the studies themselves were in the IND before Phase 3 (model_filed true)?
- Decision 16 says a harm the model predicted with a misplaced margin is counted in dose, but its dose contra row is for a target never reached. Where does a predicted-organ, misplaced-margin harm go when patients reached target and the harm was not dose-limiting (vanzacaftor liver)? Safety contra, safety same, or dose contra?
- #70 for fixed-dose combinations whose components are all class members or the shared backbone (Trikafta, Alyftrek): can a label harm attributed to the combination anchor a contra for the corrector's tox model?
- Same drug, two combinations, opposite engagement results on the same model (GLPG2737: FALCON null, PELICAN positive). Decision 6 says one drug's contra beats another drug's same; it does not say how one drug's mixed record is resolved.

Missed comparisons:

- Elexacaftor tox (NDA 212273 MultidisciplineR p.43): rat target organs stomach/testes, no liver; dog only hepatocellular hypertrophy (non-adverse). Trikafta 2019 label 5.1 'Elevated transaminases have been observed in patients with CF treated with TRIKAFTA', later liver failure. Candidate safety entry for model elexacaftor-rat-dog-tox, subject to the same #70 attribution question as safety 2.
- Lumacaftor liver: Orkambi 2015 label 5.2/6.1, 'Three patients who received ORKAMBI had liver-related serious adverse reactions ... compared to none in the placebo group', rats and dogs silent on liver (PharmR l.435). Weak because transaminase rates were similar to placebo; the draft should at least note it in safety 1.
- Galicaftor HBE model side exists (Wang 2018 J Med Chem, doi 10.1021/acs.jmedchem.7b01339) for the NCT04853368 FEV1 miss (cohort 2 vs placebo 0.9, p=0.402, verified on CT.gov). The draft says no model prediction was found; the judgement should cite this paper and say why it is not scored (combination with non-member correctors).
- PELICAN (NCT03474042, GLPG2737 on Orkambi, sweat chloride primary met, no posted results) is a same-drug match on the same HBE model; it belongs in the engagement judgement (it is mentioned) but could be its own dated tier-5 entry before the 2019 contra.
- Patient nasal epithelial cell cultures (HNE): Pranke 2017 Sci Rep, doi 10.1038/s41598-017-07504-1 (PMID 28785019), 'Correction of CFTR function in nasal epithelial cells ... predicts improvement of respiratory function by CFTR modulators' (lumacaftor/ivacaftor); later Thorax 2026 doi 10.1136/thorax-2025-223153 on sequential modulators. No parallel track in the draft.

## cystic-fibrosis-cftr-potentiators

Rule questions:

- One trial, two genotype arms predicted by the same assay: icenticaftor met the LCI2.5 primary in class III/IV but showed 'no significant efficacy' in F508del homozygotes although the model potentiated F508del CFTR (Grand 2021). Is the track same with source_calls_it partial, contra (a wrong prediction beats a right one on the same track), or untested for that arm because the model tested recombinant F508del potentiation, not F508del epithelia as monotherapy?
- Does a label whose section 12.1 (HBE) and section 14 (sweat chloride, ppFEV1) carry the two sides in separate sections count as comparison_stated and set the year under rule 6, or only a document that puts them side by side? The draft dates efficacy 4 by the 2024 label and engagement 4 by the 2025 paper for the same trial.
- A model prediction of 'greater activity' than the comparator regimens (HBE Ussing) answered by a non-inferiority primary endpoint with superiority only on a secondary (sweat chloride): is that same direction_only or source_calls_it over_predicted? Rules define over_predicted only as a source call.
- A prospective, member-drug, patient-level comparison known only from a consortium press release (CHOICES) whose model side is an unpublished organoid study: with no tier 1-3 model_src available, is the entry inadmissible, untested with the release noted, or tier 5 with model_src_tier null?
- Tracks whose model_first_use_year precedes their first entry (FRT 2012, organoids 2016) pass lint; should the opening untested entry be mandatory per track rather than per cell?

Missed comparisons:

- Prospective organoid-guided patient-level track with member drug dirocaftor: HIT-CF CHOICES, NCT06468527 (acronym CHOICES; sponsor Kors van der Ent; phase 2b placebo-controlled crossover; 41 adults selected from the HIT-CF Organoid Study; primary ppFEV1; completed 2025-06-26; no results posted). Results reported only in HIT-CF/CF Europe press material (https://www.cf-europe.eu/hit-cf-choices-cftr-modulator-trial/: lung function changes from -12% to +22% ppFEV1 'along with matching improvements in sweat chloride') and FirstWord (https://firstwordpharma.com/story/6326317: 'clinical effect proportional to in-vitro' organoid response). Tier 5 patient side, trial_unreported true; the model side (HIT-CF organoid screen) needs a tier 1-3 source not located within budget. The draft's judgement cites the wrong NCT: NCT03500263 is Proteostasis's own safety study, not CHOICES. This prospective entry on cf-intestinal-organoid-fis would precede the Kroes 2026 retrodiction.
- GLPG1837 on the HBE track before SAPHIRA1: Gees et al. 2018, Front Pharmacol, doi 10.3389/fphar.2018.01221 (open access, 2018-10-26): 'The observed activities in YFP halide assays translated well to primary cells derived from CF lungs when measured using Trans-epithelial clamp circuit (TECC).' Patient-derived primary airway cells with a member drug, so cf-hbe-ussing carries a GLPG1837 prediction (engagement, paired with Davies 2019) that the draft places only on the recombinant-assay track; it also settles the draft's open question that Van der Plas 2018 was a recombinant YFP assay.
- Checked and not missing: navocaftor (NCT03969888 results 2023-06-28; monotherapy ppFEV1 -2.2 vs -2.2 placebo, sweat chloride -8.9 vs -2.1 confirmed) has no admissible model source on Europe PMC; ORIGIN-1 organoid-guided N-of-1 trial (doi 10.1186/s13063-026-09789-y) tests ETI only, outside the member list.

## diabetic-kidney-disease-nr3c2-antagonists

Rule questions:

- Decision 10 scope: when another member's trial (ARTS-DN, 2015) has already answered the class-level question, is a later member's own first disease model (KK-Ay mouse, 2019, before that member's phase 3) a retrodiction, or a fresh prospective prediction for that member?
- Decision 16: may the stated tolerability margin come from a different system and track (GLP toxicology rat NOAEL) than the efficacy model whose regimen target is scored (DOCA-salt rat), and does a margin held in the IND but first public in the approval review count as 'stated before the trial'?
- Decision 5 converse: a rat absence claim ('no hyperkalaemia') made in non-CKD animals without RAS blockade, against patients on maximal RAS blockade with eGFR 25-60: tested prediction (contra, opposite) or 'conditions the model never tested' (untested)? And is such a contra dated to the first patient dataset that could show the harm (ARTS-DN Japan 2016, n=96, none seen; esaxerenone phase 2b 2019) or the first adequately powered one (2020)?
- Decision 15: is in-vivo blockade of an exogenous MR-agonist challenge (fludrocortisone natriuresis in healthy men) proximal, downstream, or the in-vivo analogue of 'ex-vivo blockade of a stimulus' (direct)?
- Decision 10 readout floor (models run): when a post-clinic model's only trial-mapped readout (albuminuria, creatinine clearance) is null or unmeasured and only mechanistic markers move (Kim-1/Ngal mRNA, glomerular fibrosis histology, macrophage proliferation), is the entry retro, untested or contra? The draft scored Kim-1/Ngal and fibrosis but not macrophage proliferation.
- Decision 8 scope and decision 16 (models run): is a population PK/exposure-response model fitted to the class's own phase 2b patient data a 'model system' (subjects patients, predicting patients), like in-silico QST? If so, does 'doses of both 10 and 20 mg appear safe' from simulated potassium/eGFR count as a stated margin when the source names no ceiling dose?
- Decision 8 pooling versus 'a member drug's own model' (models run): does apararenone's aldosterone-infused uninephrectomised rat share the docasalt-unx-rat track (same mineralocorticoid-excess UNx injury, same prediction) or earn its own? And under decisions 7 and 10, is a discovery paper published after the clinic (J Med Chem 2022) describing work that preceded phase 1 a retrodiction, or a prospective model dated late? The pooled mention promised on efficacy index 1 was not added.
- Decision 8 tile scope (models run): a non-diabetic albuminuric CKD rat (MWF) on a DKD tile, prediction_level class and no cross_indication flag, accepted because the class's relied-on DOCA-salt rat is also non-diabetic and the source compares with DKD patients; confirm the convention.

Missed comparisons:

- Apararenone's own model: aldosterone-infused uninephrectomised rat (primary aldosteronism model), more potent organ protection than eplerenone; Discovery of Apararenone (MT-3995), J Med Chem 2022, doi:10.1021/acs.jmedchem.2c00402 (PMID 35652647). A member drug's own model (decision 8) with no track; published after the phase 2 (2020), so retrodiction-timed unless an earlier source exists.
- Munich Wistar Fromter rat (non-diabetic CKD/albuminuria model): finerenone 10 mg/kg lowered albuminuria >40%; Front Pharmacol 2018, doi:10.3389/fphar.2018.01131 (PMID 30356804). A prospective pre-FIDELIO system with an albuminuria prediction and no track.
- Second KK-Ay paper on the kkay-hs-diabetic-mouse track: esaxerenone + olmesartan in KK-Ay mice, explicitly motivated by 'phase II and III studies' UACR data; Hypertens Res 2020, doi:10.1038/s41440-020-0495-0 (PMID 32616846), epub 2020-07-02, before ESAX-DN (2020-11-25). Supports the retrodiction reading of that track.
- Retrodictions after the clinic: esaxerenone in db/db mice, Life Sci 2025, doi:10.1016/j.lfs.2025.124008 (PMID 41067301); finerenone in a type 1 diabetes model, Biomed Pharmacother 2023, doi:10.1016/j.biopha.2023.115661 (PMID 37832406); finerenone in diabetic mice, Biochem Pharmacol 2026, doi:10.1016/j.bcp.2026.118370 (PMID 42612878). Each is a new system run after FIDELIO/ESAX-DN and belongs on its own track with retrodiction: true (decision 10).
- Patient datum omitted from efficacy patient_trials and relevant to the Dahl-rat contra dating: ARTS-DN Japan, n=96, nominally significant UACR reduction, 'no patients developed hyperkalemia'; Katayama 2017, J Diabetes Complications, doi:10.1016/j.jdiacomp.2016.11.021 (PMID 28025025), epub 2016-12-14.
- Healthy conscious dog natriuresis (urinary Na+/K+ up at >=10 mg/kg) in the FDA integrated review primary pharmacology, a second species on the natriuresis engagement track; descriptive, changes no status.
- ZSF1 obese hypertensive diabetic rat (standard T2D nephropathy model) with finerenone 10 mg/kg/day x12 weeks: UACR raised at baseline and 'Finerenone treatment did not impact kidney function but reduced renal hypertrophy'; Int J Mol Sci 2023, doi:10.3390/ijms24032536 (PMID 36768859, OA). Post-FIDELIO member use, HFpEF-framed: a retro-timed track whose kidney-function readout failed to reproduce (decision 10 contra candidate); no track and not in considered_not_added.
- Not a new system, an earlier source for the dbdb-mouse track: finerenone in db/db mice, Cells 2025, doi:10.3390/cells14050337 (PMID 40072066, PMC11898422, OA, epub 2025-02-26), 'attenuated the kidney injuries in established DN in db/db mice' with the patient benefit stated as premise; precedes Yang 2025 (2025-10-09) and Wang 2026 as opener and as the retro source.

## diabetic-macular-edema-anti-vegfa-antibodies

Rule questions:

- Decision 5 vs decision 16: a harm the animal showed (monkey nasal turbinate erosion at the clinical per-eye dose) that the regulator placed behind a stated systemic-exposure margin (42-56x, later ~3x) which held.
