- Kanamaru 2014, 'Retinal toxicity induced by small-molecule Hsp90 inhibitors in beagle dogs', J Toxicol Sci 39:59, doi:10.2131/jts.39.59 (PMID 24418710): a second animal retinal-toxicity model (dog ERG and photoreceptor histology) compared with the class's clinical visual disorders; dosed with non-member Chugai inhibitors CH5164840/CH5449302, so a sibling-drug track (product_in_model false) or out of class under decision 12; post-dates the clinic, so retro at best.
- Garon 2013, 'The HSP90 inhibitor NVP-AUY922 potently inhibits non-small cell lung cancer growth', Mol Cancer Ther, doi:10.1158/1535-7163.MCT-12-0998 (PMID 23493311): luminespib's own NSCLC cell-line and xenograft work; same nsclc-xenograft-panel track, but the proper model_src for the Sessa 2013 pairings (engagement 2, dose 2) rather than the ganetespib paper.
- Acquaviva 2012, 'Targeting KRAS-mutant non-small cell lung cancer with the Hsp90 inhibitor ganetespib', Mol Cancer Ther, doi:10.1158/1535-7163.MCT-12-0615 (PMID 23012248): the panel's KRAS-mutant prediction; Socinski 2013 cohort B (KRAS) 5.9% PFS at 16 weeks with no responses is a second patient-level miss on the same track, worth naming in efficacy entry 2.
- Synta press release 2015-10-20, 'Synta Announces Termination for Futility of Ganetespib Phase 3 GALAXY-2 Trial in Lung Cancer' (https://ir.madrigalpharma.com/node/8606; also filed on Form 8-K): tier-5 patient-side report of the GALAXY-2 failure nine months before the registry posting; sets the date of efficacy entry 4.
- Synta 8-K exhibit 99.1, June 2011 (https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1157601/000110465911033406/a11-14314_1ex99d1.htm): ASCO 2011 report of ALK-rearranged responses to ganetespib (six of eight with shrinkage, four objective responses); dates the patient result the Sang 2013 ALK model retrodicted.

## non-small-cell-lung-carcinoma-met-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Safety tracks: is a GLP rodent + non-rodent toxicology package one model system across member drugs (shared model_id; capmatinib's 2020 contra then stays sticky and headlines the cell under decision 6) or one system per drug/species pair as the draft chose (rat/monkey vs rat/dog), which lets tepotinib's same headline? Decision 8 does not decide; the drafter's own judgement says the split was made to avoid the sticky contra.
- Does a phase 1 dose-expansion cohort whose listed objective is 'preliminary efficacy' qualify as a pre-specified proof-of-concept gate (endpoint_role: poc_gate), when the trial's primary endpoint is MTD? Efficacy entry 2 (SAR125844) depends on it; otherwise the entry is untested and the strip starts at 2020.
- Decision 5 threshold: a predicted harm that appeared in 1 of 334 patients (grade 1) with the warning retained on the label: is that 'never came' (contra, magnitude opposite) or a match at lower incidence (magnitude off_by_>5x)? The capmatinib photosensitivity leg of safety entry 2 turns on it.
- Within a successful drug, when the model correctly predicted that a biomarker-negative subgroup (MET GCN<10, ORR 7-12%) would not respond, is there a neg-like status at prediction_level patient, or is same + partial the ceiling? Decision 13 defines neg only for a drug that failed.
- Dating of strengthens entries: decision 7 says every entry is dated to the earliest public comparison, but a strengthens entry changes no status; should the FDA-review year stand when a tier-1 paper a year earlier carries the same comparison (engagement 3, dose 3)?
- Decision 10 scope for efficacy 6: the residues the 2019 Ba/F3 ENU screen named (D1228, Y1230) were already reported in patients on crizotinib (non-member, 2016-2017) and savolitinib (member, Bahcall 2016) but not yet on capmatinib or tepotinib (first reports 2020). Is 'the patient result known' judged at the class level (type I MET-TKI resistance sites, so retro) or per compared member drug (so same)?
- Does rule #70 (combination-arm events anchor a contra only if the source attributes them to the class drug) apply symmetrically to same? Efficacy 8 is one real-world patient on osimertinib + savolitinib whose organoid tested the combination, whose treatment choice the source attributes to FISH rather than the DST, and whose savolitinib-alone organoid IC50 was >10 uM.

Missed comparisons:

- GEOMETRY-III (NCT04427072; ESMO 2023 abstr 1391P): the only randomised member trial in the context, capmatinib vs docetaxel 2L/3L METex14, terminated at 22/90 patients, PFS HR 0.46 (p=0.066), ORR 8/15 vs 0/6; belongs in efficacy patient_trials (no status change).
- VISION cohort B (Le 2023, 10.1016/j.xcrm.2023.101280): tepotinib in high-level MET-amplified NSCLC by liquid biopsy, ORR 41.7%, halted early; the tepotinib side of the amplification prediction is absent from patient_trials.
- Ai 2018 (10.1158/1535-7163.MCT-17-0368, epub 2017-12-13): gumarontinib/SCC244's own NSCLC patient-derived xenografts driven by MET aberration, 'robust antitumor activity at the well-tolerated doses'; a member drug's own model missing from the met-driven-xenograft track, pairs with GLORY (Yu 2023, ORR 66%).
- Gavine 2015 (10.1016/j.molonc.2014.08.015): savolitinib's only published model is gastric-cancer PDX (3/3 cMET-dysregulated models, tumour stasis); no NSCLC model found for the Lu 2021 METex14 trial, which the track should state rather than list savolitinib as if xenograft-predicted.
- Lu 2017 (10.1158/0008-5472.CAN-16-1944, epub 2017-05-18): immunocompetent mouse with the orthologous mouse METdelta14 allele, 'Met inhibition showed clinical benefit in this model', published before GEOMETRY/VISION read out; a second animal system with no track. Inhibitor not named in the abstract (the paper's patient response is to crizotinib, a non-member); untested track if a member was dosed, note-only otherwise.
- Fernandes 2023 (10.1002/1878-0261.13397): CRISPR METex14/WT isogenic human lung cells in humanised-HGF knock-in mice, 'sensitising tumours to MET-TKIs'; built after the clinic knew the answer, so a retrodiction track candidate (decision 10) if a member TKI was used.
- Hu 2025 (10.3389/fphar.2025.1685468, epub 2025-11-24): patient-derived organoids from 5 MET-amplified NSCLC patients combined with a QSP model to design personalised regimens; a patient-level ex-vivo system for this class. Abstract does not say whether PDO predictions were compared with those patients' outcomes; full text needed before scoring.
- Estevam 2025 (10.7554/elife.101882, PMC11832172, epub 2025-02-17): Ba/F3 deep mutational scan of ~5,764 MET kinase-domain variants against 11 MET inhibitors that 'validate previously identified resistance mutations'; a retro candidate on the existing baf3-engineered-met-panel track (same system, pooled), not a new model; confirm which member drugs are in the 11-inhibitor panel from the full text.
- Wang 2023 Table S6 (mmc1.pdf, read): METex14 case P-86 was treated with bozitinib (vebreltinib/PLB1001; LCO IC50 810.6, SD, scored concordant) and P-105 with crizotinib (IC50 4.95, PD, concordant); savolitinib was tested in both organoids and inactive, never given. If vebreltinib counts as a class member, P-86 is a second organoid-vs-patient comparison for the efficacy 8 note or patient_trials; otherwise the entry should say no METex14 member case exists.

## non-small-cell-lung-carcinoma-pan-her-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Decision 16 vs decision 4: when a stated tolerability margin fails (poziotinib 16 mg: 76.7% dose reductions, RDI 71.5%) but the drug is still delivered and the primary endpoint is read and missed, is dose `contra, blocked_by: toxicity` (then efficacy must be `untested`) or `contra, derived_from: efficacy` (then efficacy `contra`)? The table has no row for a margin that fails without closing the window; the draft scores both, counting one programme twice.
- Decision 4 (#53) vs the primary-endpoint rule: a registrational single-arm cohort that misses its pre-specified ORR bar with a real but small effect (ZENITH20-1: 14.8% ORR, 68.7% DCR, against a 17% null) is `contra` as a failed primary, yet #53 says 'failed' means the predicted effect was absent or reversed. Is a below-bar response `contra`, or `same` with `magnitude: off_by_>5x`?
- Decision 8: one prodrug platform, one drug, one regimen, tested first in EGFR-mutant xenografts and patients (TH-4000, 2015-17, lack of efficacy) and four years later in exon-20/HER2 patient-derived xenografts and patients (RAIN-701): one track whose first prospective test already failed, or two tracks keyed on the tumour genotype panel?
- Decision 7 vs decision 2: when a failure is first public in a tier-5 press release (Dec 2019/Jan 2020), then a conference abstract (2020), a tier-2 AdCom document (2022), a tier-1 paper (2025) and the registry (2026), the entry's year is the earliest, but which document is `src`: the highest tier with the early year, or the earliest record with `trial_unreported`?
- Decision 10: a patient-derived model built from a trial participant's sample and published alongside that cohort's responses (pyrotinib PDO/PDX 2019; NCT02535507 accrued 2015-16): what chronology evidence makes it a retrodiction when the paper does not date the model work?
- Decision 4 / #57 (models check, efficacy 12): an exploratory cohort with no predefined primary end point, outcome or hypothesis (Kris 2015 HER2 cohort) whose source calls the responses a validation: `same` + magnitude on the named response endpoint, or `untested` under #57? The schema's endpoint_role offers only primary / poc_gate; neither describes it.

Missed comparisons:

- Dacomitinib in HER2-exon-20 NSCLC: Engelman 2007 (10.1158/0008-5472.can-07-1885) states PF00299804 inhibits 'the gefitinib-resistant oncogenic ERBB2 mutation identified in lung cancers'; Kris 2015 (10.1093/annonc/mdv186, Ann Oncol, phase 2) treated 26 HER2-exon-20 patients: 3 PR (12%, 95% CI 2-30%), 0/4 HER2-amplified, naming the cell-line models as the premise (comparison stated). A patient-level comparison for a member drug in this context's population, absent from every cell; status contested (authors call it validation; 12% ORR).
- Tarloxotinib's first NSCLC test: the TH-4000 hypoxia-activated platform in EGFR-mutant models (Threshold preclinical, AACR 2015; press release https://www.biospace.com/threshold-pharmaceuticals-inc-reports-preclinical-data-suggesting-that-th-4000-may-overcome-resistance-to-conventional-egfr-tyrosine-kinase-inhibito) went into NCT02454842 (EGFR-mutant, T790M-negative NSCLC after TKI, 150 mg/m2 IV weekly, start 2015-06, TERMINATED 'Lack of efficacy', completion 2017-01, no posted results) and NCT02449681 (SCCHN, same outcome); Threshold discontinued tarloxotinib on the interim results (2016, tier 5, exact date not confirmed). Estrada-Bernal 2021 itself cites both trials at the same RP2D. A missed contra on the tarloxotinib track (or a sibling track) and model_first_use_year 2015, not 2019.
- Poziotinib regulator record: FDA ODAC briefing document, 22 Sep 2022 (https://www.fda.gov/media/161676/download; NDA 215643; ZENITH20 cohort 2 ORR 27.8%, DoR 5.1 months; 9-4 vote that benefit does not outweigh risk) and the Nov 2022 complete response letter. Tier 2 and uncited; does not flip efficacy (cohort 2 met its ORR bar) but is the HER2-cohort regulator judgement and may carry a 2022 tier-2 date for the cohort-1 miss (PDF not opened).
- Structure-based classification of EGFR mutations (Robichaux 2021, Nature, 10.1038/s41586-021-03898-1, PMC8481125): an in-silico structure-function model that 'retrospectively predict[s] patient outcomes following treatment with EGFR inhibitors', built on 16,715 patients and including poziotinib exon-20 data. A retrodiction track (model_kind in_silico, status retro) for the exon-20 context with no entry.
- Earlier tier-1 record on the dacomitinib del19/L858R track: Janne 2014 (Lancet Oncol, ARCHER 1017 phase 2, first-line EGFR-mutant, ORR about 76%), fetched by neither drafter nor checker; under decision 7 it likely moves efficacy 4 (and the paired dose/engagement entries) from 2017 to 2014.
- ZENITH20 cohort-1 earliest records, the dating sources for efficacy 6: Spectrum topline announcement (Dec 2019/Jan 2020; OncLive 2020-01-03 https://www.onclive.com/view/poziotinib-misses-primary-endpoint-in-egfr-exon-20mutant-nsclc; SEC exhibit https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/831547/000083154720000003/a2020spectrumcorporatede.htm) and the AACR 2020 oral abstract Le 2025 names as the first presentation. EDGAR full-text search returned nothing, so the exact press-release date is unconfirmed.

## non-small-cell-lung-carcinoma-vegfr-multikinase-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Dose when the model stated no exposure, occupancy or regimen target but the trial gave the full clinical dose of the modelled regimen: untested (nothing to compare, as the draft scores the H460 and RET tracks) or 'reached' with the efficacy-derived status (as it scores the CT26/BNL track)? The dose table presumes a target and does not say.
- Single-arm phase 2 whose primary ORR (16%, 4/25) the authors call 'relatively low' with PFS 7.3 months: does same + source_calls_it partial require the trial's pre-specified ORR bar to be met, or does any activity in the predicted direction on the primary endpoint suffice? If the bar governs, Hida 2019 is contra.
- A 100% biomarker-selected trial (RET fusion-positive) scored at prediction_level patient inside the Unselected context: does the RET-fusion track belong to a separate 'RET fusion-positive' population context, and does a patient-level prediction resting on RET rather than VEGFR inhibition belong to the VEGFR multikinase class at all? The >=80% rule covers only the reverse direction.
- A cross_indication model (CT26 colon / BNL hepatoma isografts, no lung tumour ever tested) as a track in the NSCLC context: is a contra admissible when the model never tested the disease, or should cross_indication entries cap at untested here with the contra scored in the CRC/HCC contexts?
- A post-failure lung model that tests a different regimen (adds radiotherapy to lenvatinib + anti-PD-L1): is it a retrodiction of the LEAP result under decision 10 (contra for not reproducing it) or a fresh prospective prediction for the triple combination?
- A second model run on an existing track that leaves the status untested (Liu 2026 lenvatinib after Yuan 2022 famitinib on llc-syngeneic-c57bl6-rt-checkpoint): decision 7 writes a new entry only on a status change or a second miss with strengthens: true. Does a new member-drug run of the same system get its own untested entry, or a note plus patient_trials on the opening entry? (models check)

Missed comparisons:

- Famitinib murine lung-cancer syngeneic model of famitinib + radiotherapy + immune checkpoint inhibitor (Thorac Cancer 2022, PMID 36281217, doi 10.1111/1759-7714.14689): a lung model for a member drug with NSCLC patient data on the camrelizumab + famitinib doublet (JITC 2024, PMID 38388167, doi 10.1136/jitc-2023-007227, first-line PD-L1>=1% ORR 53.7%; Cancer Immunol Immunother 2024, PMID 38727837, doi 10.1007/s00262-024-03715-4, post chemo-IO ORR 7.5%) and three terminated phase 3s. The draft says no famitinib NSCLC model exists; this track is absent (pairing is partial because the model regimen adds RT).
- Famitinib in EGFR-mutant NSCLC cell-line xenografts NCI-H1975 and PC-9 with almonertinib/HS-10296 (Thorac Cancer 2021, PMID 33656275, doi 10.1111/1759-7714.13902): a member-drug entry for the nsclc-human-xenograft-immunodeficient-mouse track (untested; the camrelizumab + famitinib phase 2 EGFR-mutant subgroup n=6 ORR 33% is the only patient side).
- Lenvatinib in an immunocompetent C57BL/6 lung adenocarcinoma model with RT + anti-PD-L1 (Cancer Biol Ther 2026, PMID 41481002, doi 10.1080/15384047.2025.2610526): the first lung-specific syngeneic model of lenvatinib + checkpoint blockade, published after LEAP-006/007/008 failed, reporting potentiation; a retrodiction candidate on the combination track (decision 10: contra if it does not reproduce the human result), subject to rule question 5.
- Patient trials the famitinib arm omits from patient_trials: NCT04346381 basket cohorts above (JITC 2024; CII 2024). Checked and excluded as non-member drugs: RET-fusion NSCLC PDX/cell models (Dis Model Mech 2020, PMID 33318047; cabozantinib, RXDX-105) and lung adenocarcinoma PDOs (Clin Cancer Res 2021, PMID 34083237; pralsetinib, poziotinib).

## open-angle-glaucoma-rock-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- ROCKET-1 (Study 301) failed its primary endpoint, which was non-inferiority of mean IOP to timolol, although IOP fell significantly. The model predicted IOP lowering with no comparator margin. Does an active-comparator NI primary that fails while the absolute endpoint responds count as an efficacy 'contra', or is it untested for the model? The draft kept it as a status-neutral patient_trial.
- Decision 16 margin_stated: the 2017 PharmR states NOAEL margins of 8x/2x for the chronic studies but a corneal-haze NOEL margin of 1. Is margin_stated judged on the NOAEL or on the most sensitive finding? Neither margin was shown to be public before Phase 2. Status 'same' holds either way.
- When the system relied on is an IOP-lowering model in normotensive animals, should a failed sibling-member drug with model data only in a conference abstract (ATS907 ARVO 2011) be admissible on the model side (tier 1 needs DOI/PMID)?
- efficacy 4: an undirected genotype hypothesis ('may influence response') meets an underpowered null (aOR 1.31, CI 0.42-4.10). Is that a failed prediction (contra) or no patient result (untested)? The rules decide neither undirected predictions nor underpowered nulls outside terminated trials.

Missed comparisons:

- ATS907 had public model data: Hsu & Kengatharan, ARVO 2011, 'Safety and Efficacy of a Novel Topical Rho Kinase Inhibitor ATS907 in Normotensive Cynomolgus Monkeys' (https://iovs.arvojournals.org/article.aspx?articleid=2360870): single-dose IOP -3.7/-4.65 mmHg vs -2.43 latanoprost, plus a rabbit abstract. The draft's note says 'no public model data' for ATS907; this is wrong. The Phase 2 failure (NCT01520116 status unknown, NCT01668524 no results) is known only from the Wang & Chang 2014 review, so it is not scorable without a tier 1-5 patient source. If one is found, ATS907 would be a contra on a normotensive-monkey IOP model.
- DE-104 Phase 2 trials NCT00650338, NCT00657579, NCT00656240, NCT00868894 are registered with no posted results. The discontinuation for a missed primary endpoint (late 2010) appears only in the review; no Santen tier 5 document was found in budget, and no DE-104 model data was found.
- Ripasudil (K-115, Kowa; approved Japan 2014) is a ROCK inhibitor for POAG/OHT with its own rabbit/monkey IOP models (e.g. Isobe 2014, Curr Eye Res) and Japanese patient trials (Tanihara 2013, Am J Ophthalmol); no track if it is in the class. Not verified in budget.
- AR-12286 (Aerie's earlier ROCK inhibitor) had monkey IOP data and a Phase 2 patient trial (Williams 2011, Am J Ophthalmol) before netarsudil; a member drug's own model with no track. Not verified in budget.

## osteoarthritis-mapk14-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Conference abstracts have no tier: the ACR 2012 late-breaking abstract (Arthritis Rheum 64 Suppl 10, sponsor-authored, unreviewed, no DOI) and the EFIC 2011 abstract (Eur J Pain Suppl, has a DOI) are neither tier 1 papers nor tier 5 press releases. The draft scored the ACR abstract as tier 5 with trial_unreported true; is that the rule?
- Safety: a tox-predicted harm (FX005 cartilage damage at HED >=10 mg) where the only trial (45 mg, 12 weeks, no imaging) could not detect the cartilage endpoint but could and did assess the synovitis component clinically (local tolerability HA-like). Untested as drafted, or contra/opposite for the assessable component? And if the repeat-dose tox post-dated the trial while the clinic never measured the endpoint, is it a prospective prediction, a retrodiction, or neither?
- Evidence rule 2 requires a tier 1-3 model_src on a tier 4-5 entry, but the only source for FX005's rat toxicology is the SEC S-1 itself (model_src_tier null). Lint accepts it; may an untested safety entry stand on a tier-5 model side when no higher-tier model source exists?
- A sponsor pipeline document silently dropping an indication after a completed, unreported trial (PH-797804 OA): is that an admissible tier-5 failure signal, or nothing until a result is stated? The dose cell's untested depends on the answer.
- Dose entry 1 carries trial_unreported true on a source_tier 1 entry; the brief ties trial_unreported to tier 5. Should trial_unreported be allowed on any tier when the relevant patient trial itself is unreported?
- Engagement on a cross-indication track where the human PD readout (ex-vivo LPS whole-blood cytokines in dosed volunteers, Koch 2007) was never a readout of the rodent model (CIA/adjuvant arthritis scores): is a title that lists both findings enough for comparison_stated true and same, or does decision 4 require the model itself to have used the readout (untested, with the human PD re-homed on the rat-endotoxin-tnf-pd track)? (CHECKER_BRIEF_v3 / models, engagement 3)

Missed comparisons:

- ARRY-797's own model, no track: Koch et al. 2007, 'ARRY-797, a selective, potent inhibitor of p38 active in animal models of rheumatoid arthritis, inhibits ex vivo IL-1, PGE2 and TNF production in normal human subjects', Inflamm Res 2007;56(Suppl 3):S376-S377 (abstract book doi 10.1007/BF03353884; relayed by Mavers 2009 Curr Rheumatol Rep doi 10.1007/s11926-009-0054-9: rodent CIA/adjuvant arthritis plus ex-vivo LPS whole-blood PGE2/IL-1/TNF inhibition up to 100% in dosed healthy subjects). A member-drug engagement comparison (readout_tier direct) dated 2007, two years before Hope 2009; abstract body not retrieved (ResearchGate metadata and citing text only).
- Patient-side engagement on the rat-iodoacetate track, no entry: Kivitz 2012 ACR abstract L1 reports ARRY-797 cut serum COMP 10% and CTX-I 38% vs placebo at week 4 in knee-OA patients (same URL as efficacy entry 2). Brown 2008 predicted joint-degeneration inhibition; the cartilage/bone degradation biomarkers moving in patients is a downstream engagement comparison the engagement cell (currently healthy-volunteer endotoxin only) should carry as a 2012 entry.
- Not a model, a sponsor record the dose/efficacy notes could cite: Pfizer pipeline PDF dated 2011-11-10 (https://cdn.pfizer.com/pfizercom/research/pipeline/2011_1110/pipeline_2011_1110.pdf) lists PH-797804 for COPD Phase 2 only, nine months after NCT01102660 completed (2011-02), so the OA indication was dropped without a stated result. It does not report the outcome, so no status changes.
- No further model system for this class found (checked_by CHECKER_BRIEF_v3 / models). The only other Array ARRY-797 model abstracts are oncology: Wright/Winski et al., Blood 2006;108(11):3478 'ARRY-797 ... Inhibits LPS-Induced IL-6 and In Vivo Growth of RPMI-8226 Human Multiple Myeloma Cells' (https://ashpublications.org/blood/article/108/11/3478/128129) and Ann Oncol 2007;18 Suppl 4 xenograft abstract. The Blood LPS-IL-6 result (in vitro or in vivo not known from the title) would be ARRY-797's own model_src for the existing rat-endotoxin-tnf-pd track, paired with the Koch 2007 human ex-vivo PD; not a new system.

## osteoarthritis-ntrk1-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Dose, decision 4: when the trial paper itself states after the failure that plasma levels were 'well above the IC50 reported in pre-clinical models' (total drug), the dose was chosen on a toxicity ceiling with the pre-clinical dosing data 'on file', and the unbound trough (111 nM) fell below the in-vitro IC50 (155 nM), is this case 3 (reached, failed, abandoned: contra derived_from efficacy) or the post-hoc PK/PD clause (untested, source_calls_it scaling)? Which exposure measure, total or unbound, peak or trough, counts as 'reached'?
- Class boundary, decision 12: GZ389988's own sponsor reports similar IC50s for TrkA, TrkB, TrkC and CSF1R (0.89/0.38/0.5/1.3 nM) and the editorial raises CSF1R as a contributor to its analgesia, yet the tile lists it as an NTRK1 inhibitor. Does a member stay in the class when its own sources describe it as pan-Trk/CSF1R, and is its injection-site joint inflammation an on-target harm (pooled) or a formulation harm?
- Human genetic models, decision 8/4: Watt 2019 puts human NTRK1 loss-of-function (insensitivity to pain) beside the failed ASP7962 trial and attributes the gap to the completeness and duration of blockade. Is a lifelong complete loss-of-function ever an admissible model of a 4-week partial inhibitor, and if so is this pairing untested (conditions the genotype never tested) or contra?
- Track identity, decision 8: Flannery's rat IA biocompatibility study used naive rat knees, while the efficacy model is rat MIA knee OA. Is a naive-joint tolerability study the same system as the rat MIA track for safety (one species, one route, one compound programme), or its own model_id?
- Human genetic models, decision 4 symmetry (models check): if ASP7962's failure cannot contradict the CIPA genotype / TrkA-null tracks because a partial 4-week inhibitor never produced the genotype's condition (proposed untested), can GZ389988A's met 4-week primary confirm them? Krupka cites the genotype only in its intro (ref 25), a bare-citation (b) pairing; by the same logic that pairing should also be untested, which leaves both genetic tracks untested throughout.
- Track identity, decision 8 (models check): the Flannery 2015 rat IA biocompatibility statement is now the model side of two contra entries, rat-mia-knee-oa safety index 2 (adjudicated) and rat-naive-knee-ia-tolerability safety index 5 (this check). Naive-knee tolerability is a different experiment from MIA knee OA, so the new track is the better home and the rat-MIA safety contra should revert to untested; whichever is chosen, the comparison must be counted once.

Missed comparisons:

- Flannery CR et al. 2015, 'Efficacy of a novel, locally delivered TrkA inhibitor in preclinical models of OA and joint pain', Osteoarthritis Cartilage 23(Suppl 2):A45-A46, DOI 10.1016/j.joca.2015.02.100 (OARSI abstract, April 2015): GZ389988's own rat MIA single-IA-injection model, rat PGPS flare model and rat IA biocompatibility study. Cited by Krupka 2019 (ref 28: 'In rat models of OA and joint pain, GZ389988 showed a significant relief of local knee pain'), so the GZ389988A efficacy pairing is comparison_stated with product_in_model true (same, subordinate to ASP7962 contra on the same track), and the rat biocompatibility silence on joint inflammation is the model side of the safety contra proposed above. The draft said this abstract could not be located.
- Human NTRK1 loss-of-function (HSAN IV / congenital insensitivity to pain) as a human_genetic track: Watt 2019 (DOI 10.1016/j.joca.2019.05.029, full text) sets it beside the failed trial: 'Individuals with mutations of the TrkA receptor, either in humans or in pre-clinical models, have insensitivity to pain, suggesting that TrkA is a valid analgesic target', then attributes the gap to the degree and duration of blockade. No track in the draft; likely untested (conditions the genotype never tested) rather than contra; see rule question 3.
- NGF-receptor knockout mouse (Watt 2019 ref 7): cited in the same discussion as pre-clinical evidence against the 'more complete blockade needed' explanation; a mouse genetic model placed beside the patient result with no track.
- GZ389988 first-in-human study NCT02424942 (Genzyme/Sanofi, Phase 1, 28 knee-OA patients, ascending single IA doses, April 2015 to July 2016): secondary endpoints were plasma and synovial-fluid PK and WOMAC pain over 12 weeks; no results posted, no publication found. This, not NCT02845271, was the escalation study; it is the only patient-side PK source for the IA member and should be recorded on the dose/engagement cells as unreported.
- ASP7962 Phase 1 healthy-volunteer studies NCT01981928 and NCT02136316 (Watt 2019 full text: single doses up to 240 mg, favourable safety; 100 mg BID was the highest dose with safety data when Phase 2 started): healthy-volunteer PK counts for the dose cell under v3; no results posted, no publication, so no status change, but the draft does not cite them.
- Not searched for budget: Astellas, Sanofi and Purdue discontinuation notices (tier 5) for ASP7962, GZ389988A and VM902A; none would change a status since the tier 1 trial papers and the registry termination record already set them.

## ovarian-cancer-parp1-2-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Safety: when an FDA review says animal findings were 'consistent with' the dose-limiting harms but explicitly notes a common patient harm the species could have shown (rucaparib ALT/AST, 74% incidence) was absent, is that same + partial or contra (silence contradicted)?
- Efficacy: does outcome_tested apply when the trial primary endpoint (PFS) is met but later OS shows detriment (ARIEL4) — i.e. is PFS a 'surrogate the model predicted' in oncology, and should an OS detriment ever move an efficacy cell?
- model_change: is it meaningful on an entry that follows a same (not contra) entry with a different model, or should lint reject model_change: true unless the previous entry is contra?

Missed comparisons:

- ARIEL4 (rucaparib vs chemotherapy, BRCA-mutant relapsed ovarian; NCT02855944, results posted 2022-02-11): primary invPFS met (Kristeleit, Lancet Oncol 2022), but final OS 19.4 vs 25.4 months (Lancet Oncol 2025, PIIS1470-2045(24)00674-0); FDA treatment indication voluntarily withdrawn June 2022. Status stays same on the primary endpoint, but efficacy should carry this as a patient_trials item / outcome_tested: negative.
- NOVA final OS (gBRCA HR ~0.85, not significant; non-gBRCA OS detriment) led to the 2022 restriction of niraparib 2L maintenance to gBRCA; relevant to outcome_tested, not to the PFS-based status.
- Stenoparib (2X-121, member) phase 2 NCT03878849 in platinum-resistant ovarian cancer, DRP-selected not BRCA-selected; sponsor press releases only (Allarity 2025), positive in tone, outside the BRCA/HRD population; no failure to add.

## paroxysmal-nocturnal-hemoglobinuria-anti-c5-antibodies

Rule questions:

- Dose: the monkey-based paper claimed a recycling antibody needs 'a much lower trough' than the ~99 µg/mL a conventional antibody needs, yet patients needed ~100 µg/mL. The regimen still worked. Is a wrong qualitative exposure claim with a working regimen 'same' with magnitude opposite or unquantified, or 'contra' (target too low)? Decision 16 and the dose table do not cover this case.
- Efficacy for a follow-on member (ravulizumab) rested on non-inferiority to a non-member (eculizumab) plus an engagement assay. Does that count as an efficacy model, or as 'no disease model relied on' (empty list)? It is close to the cross-indication case but sits in the same indication.
- model_change: is it meant only for turning a cell back after a contra, or also for any switch of model between entries? The draft uses it for both, and lint does not check it.
- Safety cell with two distinct harms (meningococcal infection; DTDC hypersensitivity), each predicted by a different model: should these be separate entries scored independently, or one entry per change of best-model status as now?

Missed comparisons:

- Pozelimab + cemdisiran (ACCESS-1, NCT05133531; Regeneron press release and Lancet 2026) was positive against ravulizumab in PNH. The combination includes an siRNA, so it is optional in patient_trials and does not change status. Source: https://pmlive.com/pharma_news/regeneron-shares-positive-late-stage-results-for-poze-cemdi-in-rare-blood-disorder-pnh/
- Tesidolumab: Novartis stopped development in favour of iptacopan, a strategic choice and not an efficacy failure. Worth a note so it is not read as a failure. Source: NCT02534909 / Novartis trial results.
- Pozelimab's FDA review in the bundle (BLA 761339) is for CHAPLE disease, not PNH. It cannot serve as a PNH source, and the draft correctly does not use it.
- COMMODORE 1 C5-polymorphism cohort (FDA crovalimab integrated review 2024, BLA 761388): one subject 'discontinued the study treatment on or after 24 weeks of treatment due to lack of efficacy'. It is a later patient result for the c5-r885h-variant-hemolysis-assay track and has no entry of its own. The cohort's genotype-level hemolysis data should be sought before it is scored.

## plasma-cell-myeloma-psmb5-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Dose with no model-stated exposure target: delanzomib was dosed to an MTD set by unpredicted toxicity (rash, thrombocytopenia), showed dose-related PBMC proteasome inhibition (45% at RD), and failed. Is that 'reached, failed, abandoned' (dose contra derived_from efficacy; efficacy contra) or 'never reached because of toxicity; no stated margin' (both untested)? The table assumes a stated target.
- margin_stated across tracks: the ixazomib dose entry (model_id mm1s-scid-xenograft) carries margin_stated: true from the GLP rat/dog NOAEL, a different system with its own model_id in safety. Must a stated margin be scored on the stating system's own track (a dose entry for ixazomib-rat-dog-glp-tox), and does a margin first made public in the 2015 FDA review (study pre-dating the IND) count as 'stated before the trial'?
- Population axis: the brief defines population as genotype / biomarker / pathogen, else Unselected; the draft wrote 'Unselected (relapsed/refractory)'. Is line of therapy a population split? If not, TOURMALINE-MM2's missed primary (2020) is a contra on the ixazomib track and MM3/MM4 belong in patient_trials.
- A numerically favourable but non-significant primary (TOURMALINE-MM2: HR 0.83, p=0.073, +13.5 months median PFS): contra under 'did the primary endpoint respond as predicted', or is there room for same with source_calls_it unresolved? Decision 13 says judge on the data, not the sponsor's reading, but does not say which way a missed significance threshold falls.
- Regulatory reversal on one trial: the CHMP's 2016-05-26 negative opinion on TOURMALINE-MM1 (tier 2) was reversed on re-examination four months later. Does the interim negative opinion create a dated contra entry, or does only the regulator's final decision set the status when two tier-2 records of the same trial disagree?
- Safety dating with imported_from: when the first public contradiction of a safety claim comes from another indication (Infante 2016 solid tumours; Gallerani 2013 mixed), does the entry take that earlier year with imported_from, or the first same-disease date the draft used? My two year corrections assume the earlier date.
- Patient-level tracks vs the primary-endpoint rule (decision 4): an ex-vivo patient-cell prediction ('bortezomib-exposed patients still respond') is naturally tested by response rate within a prior-exposure cohort, which in a phase 1 is a secondary objective. Does a sponsor-designed expansion cohort stratified by prior-PI exposure (Kumar 2014, NCT00963820) count as a pre-specified poc_gate, or must a patient-level efficacy track wait for a subgroup of a primary endpoint (TOURMALINE-MM1 prior-PI-exposed 69.5% on PFS)?
- One patient datum on two model tracks: Vogl 2017's 9% PR anchors a contra on both delanzomib-mm-xenograft and delanzomib-lagk-scid-hu-pdx (two systems, same single-agent prediction). The headline de-duplicates (decision 6) but per-track contra counts record one drug failure twice. Should the statistics de-duplicate per drug-trial datum, or is 'two models both wrong' the intended count?
- In-silico dose models built and used before publication: the ixazomib popPK was run during phase 2 (ClinPharmR line 236) and dosed MM1 at fixed 4 mg from 2012, but the earliest public record is the Nov-2014 epub. Decision 7 opens the track at the earliest public record; should model_first_use_year instead carry the regulator-documented first use with a proxy flag?

Missed comparisons:

- Delanzomib patient PD on the delanzomib-mm-xenograft track: Gallerani 2013, Eur J Cancer 49:290-296, doi 10.1016/j.ejca.2012.09.009, PMID 23058787, epub 2012-10-08: 'Proteasome inhibition in peripheral blood mononuclear cells was dose related in MM patients; it was of 45.4±11.5% at the RD.' Engagement same (direction_only) dated 2012; also a dose datum (RD 1.5 mg/m2 twice weekly, MTD set by rash) and safety data (rash 53%, dose-limiting; no significant peripheral neuropathy).
- TOURMALINE-MM2, NCT01850524, ixazomib-Rd vs placebo-Rd in transplant-ineligible newly diagnosed myeloma: primary PFS missed, HR 0.830 (95% CI 0.676-1.018), p=0.073, median 35.3 vs 21.8 months. Facon 2021 Blood, doi 10.1182/blood.2020008787, PMID 33763699; topline press release Takeda 2020-03-10 (Healio 2020-03-11). A 2020 contra on the mm1s-scid-xenograft efficacy track, outranking the 2015 same, if newly diagnosed patients belong to the context (rule question 3).
- TOURMALINE-MM3 (post-transplant maintenance, PFS positive, Lancet 2019) and TOURMALINE-MM4 (non-transplant maintenance, PFS positive, 2020): same-direction newly-diagnosed trials for patient_trials if that population is in context; DOIs not verified in this check.
- EMA record of the same trial the FDA SumR scores: CHMP negative opinion 2016-05-26 (final PFS HR 0.82, p=0.0548 read as not demonstrating benefit), positive opinion on re-examination 2016-09-15, conditional MA 2016-11-21. EPAR Ninlaro EMEA/H/C/003844; EMA Q&A https://www.ema.europa.eu/et/media/14960; Takeda release 2016-05-27 via https://myelomabeacon.org/pr/2016/05/27/ninlaro-ixazomib-chmp-negative-opinion/; Tzogani 2019 ESMO Open. A tier-2 document the efficacy cell should cite (rule question 5).
- Patient-derived ex vivo MM cells, a patient-level system with no model_id in the draft: Piva 2008 CD138+ explants from untreated and bortezomib-treated patients; Chauhan 2010 (doi 10.1182/blood-2010-04-276626) and Chauhan 2011 (doi 10.1158/1078-0432.CCR-11-0476) 'MM cells resistant to conventional and bortezomib therapies'. Patient side: Richardson 2014 (88% prior bortezomib) and Kumar 2014 (cohorts by prior bortezomib/carfilzomib exposure); the refractory-subgroup ORR is not in the abstracts, so the track probably closes untested, but it existed and was used for this class.
- Oprozomib halt source: NCT01416428's own whyStopped carries the 'program evaluation identified that the safety profile and pharmacokinetic (PK) characteristics of the formulation ... required further optimization' statement; the draft attributes it only to NCT01881789 (results posted 2021-03-03, confirmed).

## plasma-cell-myeloma-tnfrsf17-directed-depleters

Rule questions:

- Safety: when the model showed the harm but its own report called it non-adverse (Blenrep corneal necrosis 'considered non-adverse') and patients had severe keratopathy, is that a predicted harm that held (same, direction_only) or a stated 'no adverse finding' contradicted (contra)? Rules decide neither.
- Efficacy: a confirmatory phase 3 vs an active comparator that misses (DREAMM-3) while single-agent activity is reproduced: is the comparator 'a condition the model never tested' (untested) or a primary-endpoint miss (contra)? The rule list names untested only when the trial source attributes the result to untested conditions; GSK attributed it to nothing.
- Engagement: may a phase 1 whose primary endpoint is safety supply the engagement entry when its readout (marrow plasma-cell clearance) is effectively the efficacy readout of the xenograft?
- Class-level post-marketing harms (CAR-T T-cell malignancy boxed warning) shared with CD19 products: score in this context or import?
- Efficacy index 4 (ide-cel response by tumour BCMA level): a patient-selection prediction scored on a phase 1 subgroup of a secondary end point (ORR; primary was safety) with endpoint_role null. Should such patient-level selection entries require endpoint_role poc_gate, or are they exempt from the secondary-endpoint rule?
- Dating when the patient result (ASH 2017 abstract) is public before the model's first publication (Friedman 2018) but the model data were filed pre-trial: may the opening untested entry be dated earlier than its publication to keep the strip ordered?

Missed comparisons:

- DREAMM-3 primary PFS miss (HR 1.03) was public 2022-11-07 via GSK press release / SEC 6-K (https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1131399/000165495422014760/a4558f.htm); patient_trials cites only the 2026 final analysis DOI with a title as its quote and should cite the 2022 record, the first public date.
- Ide-cel insertional-oncogenesis prediction: Abecma SBRA states integration-site analysis 'does not preferentially integrate in or near genes of concern for oncogenic transformation' and an IL-2-independent growth assay 'showed no signs of uncontrolled cellular proliferation' (ex_vivo model). FDA required a class boxed warning for T-cell malignancies, including CAR+ cases, on BCMA- and CD19-directed CAR-T incl. Abecma (Jan-Apr 2024; https://www.onclive.com/view/fda-requires-boxed-warning-for-risk-of-t-cell-malignancies-with-approved-car-t-cell-therapies). A candidate cell-therapy safety entry (contra or untested, depending on whether any ide-cel case was CAR+); not verified at product level here.

## primary-biliary-cholangitis-nr1h4-agonists

Rule questions:

- Decision 4 dose case 2 ('reached and the drug worked') on a PD-model-only track that states no human exposure, occupancy or regimen target: does 'reached' require a stated target (then untested, as the drafter's own quote shows), or does engagement at a tolerated, effective dose suffice? And if dose is same on that basis, must efficacy carry the matching same for that member (tropifexor met its GGT primary; no efficacy entry)?
- Dating conflict: the drafter brief's rule 6 lists registry posting, epub, label and print; RULES_V3 decision 7 adds 'the year of the earliest admissible source that makes the comparison'. Is a tier-5 press release (INTREPID topline 2020-05-06) a dating event? If yes the INTREPID entries date 2020, not 2021.
- Is a conference abstract (AASLD/EASL, journal supplement with a DOI) an admissible tier for patient-side efficacy when it is the only record (cilofexor PBC NCT02943447 posts no efficacy outcome)? The source-tier table does not list it.
- Safety 'askable silence': does 'measurable in the model species at the time' mean the species can show the symptom class (rodent scratching), or that the drug-induced harm has been shown in that species? FXR-agonist pruritus has no rodent precedent and the IL-31 signal needed a humanized-liver mouse (PMID 35686937). The answer sets safety 2 and safety 3 together: both contra or both untested.
- Is a harm counted once across tracks within the safety cell (the drafter kept the Mdr2-/- track untested because the pruritus miss was scored on the PD track), or does each track's silence score on its own? Decision 16 counts once only across dose and safety.
- strengthens: true: reserved for a second miss that removes the first miss's excuse (#127), or also for a second member's match of the same status on the same track (Hollenback 2024 after Badman 2020 on rodent-fxr-pd)?
- Model-side sibling-drug allowance (product_in_model: false): does it extend to obeticholic acid, which the class table excludes from this tile, when the patient side is a member (IL-31 humanized-liver mouse dosed with OCA; cilofexor in patients)?
- Species on one surgical model: is a piglet BDL a separate model_id from the rat BDL track named 'rodent-bdl', or the same system?

Missed comparisons:

- Humanized-liver mouse / human-hepatocyte IL-31 model: Xu et al., Hepatology 2023;77(1) (PMID 35686937, first pub 2022-07-17). OCA raised human IL-31 mRNA and serum human IL-31 in humanized-liver mice; cilofexor dose-dependently raised IL-31 in NASH patients, higher with grade 2-3 pruritus; PBC/PSC baseline IL-31 correlated with itch scores. A safety-cell retrodiction track for the class's dose-limiting harm (model-side drug is OCA, a non-member; rule question).
- Piglet BDL model: Xiao et al., Liver Int 2021 (PMID 33894097, first pub 2021-05-18): tropifexor suppressed BDL-induced injury, fibrosis and ductular reaction with portal FGF19 up. Patient side is biliary atresia; paired with the tropifexor PBC result it is a retro entry that predates Kilavuz (2021-10) and is a different species from the rat BDL track (model_id question). The draft names it in a judgement but gives it no entry.
- Missed comparison rather than model: tropifexor's PBC trial met its registry primary, GGT fold change (NCT02516605 results 2019-11-13; Schramm 2022, PMID 36267872), yet no efficacy entry scores it on any prospective track while the dose cell scores 'reached, drug worked' on it. Either an efficacy entry belongs on the track the dose cell uses, or the cell should state that tropifexor went into PBC on no public disease model.
- Not found, consistent with the draft: no peer-reviewed INTREPID or cilofexor-PBC paper on PubMed/Europe PMC (searched 2026-11-05). Could not verify: Enanta 2020-05-06 press release (Business Wire and Enanta IR refuse curl; EDGAR full-text search returns no 8-K), so the patient_trials quote on efficacy 3 is unverified; the AASLD 2019 cilofexor abstract was not opened; all papers read at abstract level.

## prostate-cancer-ar-antagonists

Rule questions:

- Safety: when the filed animal toxicity (dog convulsions at a Cmax at or near the human Cmax) predicts a harm at clinical exposure and patients show it at 0.2%, is that 'same' in direction, or does the magnitude need to be recorded as over_predicted?
- Rule 6 opener: when the model's first use (2010 dog study) became public only in the same document that carries the comparison (2018 FDA review), is an untested opener at model_first_use_year still required? The draft skipped it and lint did not flag it.
- Population mismatch for a member drug on its own model: if a castration-resistant (NHA-naive) xenograft is paired with a post-abiraterone phase 3 failure (proxalutamide), is the cell untested under 'conditions the model never tested', or contra?
- Patient-level parallel tracks (efficacy 6): when the model predicts a resistance mutation and the patient datum is an exploratory cfDNA finding in progressing patients (not a trial endpoint, no carrier vs non-carrier comparison), does it count as an admissible 'primary' efficacy comparison, or should it be untested?

Missed comparisons:

- Proxalutamide (pruxelutamide, a class member) has an in-vivo model: Zhou 2020 Eur J Cancer, doi:10.1016/j.ejca.2020.04.013 (PMID 32460179), 'The efficacy of GT0918 was investigated in a castration-resistant xenograft model' plus phase 1 (PSA>=50% in 2/16). The draft's claim that its preclinical work is cell lines only is wrong. Kintor announced (Sept 2020 IDMC; tier 5, e.g. Kintor 2020 interim results, en.kintor.com.cn/news/131.html) that the 2L+ mCRPC phase 3 missed interim rPFS. It needs its own model_id entry in efficacy: probably untested (model never tested post-abiraterone/docetaxel disease), otherwise contra. The trial population was not verified.
- Darolutamide's own model (castration-resistant VCaP xenograft; FDA review NDA 212099, lines ~1364-1378) is only mentioned in a judgement. As a different system, it should get its own model_id entries (efficacy vs ARADES NCT01317641 / ARAMIS; safety, where mouse brain:plasma was low and ARAMIS seizures were 0.2% in both arms).
- Rezvilutamide (SHR3680): CHART phase 3 in high-volume mHSPC was positive, but no published in-vivo model was found, so no entry is needed. TRC-253 (JNJ-63576253): preclinical xenografts in Branch 2021 MCT (doi:10.1158/1535-7163.MCT-20-0510); the phase 1/2a primary endpoint was DLT, so it is unscored, as the draft says.
- Enzalutamide's own F876L resistance models: Korpal 2013 Cancer Discov (doi:10.1158/2159-8290.CD-13-0142, PMID 23842682; resistant LNCaP-derived lines) and Balbas 2013 eLife (doi:10.7554/eLife.00499; mutagenesis screen) predicted F876L resistance; Azad 2015 Clin Cancer Res (doi:10.1158/1078-0432.CCR-14-2666) reported F876L in cfDNA of an enzalutamide-progressing patient and AR aberrations predicting lower PSA response. They could join the arn509-resistant-cell-lines-f876l track (product_in_model per drug) if enzalutamide is a member.

## prostate-cancer-cyp17a1-ar-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- When the model stated no exposure or regimen target, do the derived dose rows ('reached, failed, abandoned' -> contra derived_from efficacy; 'reached, drug worked' -> same) apply at the clinically chosen dose, or does dose stay untested? The draft applies both readings to galeterone's 2,550 mg/d (dose[1] untested, dose[5] contra); if the derived rows apply, the LAPC4 track also needs a 2015 dose same it lacks, and the ODM-204 'PK never reached' contra rests on the sponsor's verdict with no model target.
- Dating a failure first public in a press release / SEC filing: ARMOR3-SV futility was public 2016-07-26 (tier 5) but the draft turns the track contra in 2019 on the tier-1 paper because rule 6's date list omits press releases while rule 2 says tier-5 failures must be found. Should the track carry a 2016 tier-5 contra with trial_unreported true, with 2019 as the confirming entry?
- comparison_stated for a trial paper that states the model-derived hypothesis in its own words ('galeterone ... induces AR protein degradation. We hypothesized ...') but shows no model data (Taplin 2019): statement or pairing under rule 1?
- Toxicity-driven early discontinuation confounding the primary read (Madan 2020: median 2 cycles vs a 12-week PSA50 primary, 1/17): is efficacy contra on the 'reached, failed' row, or untested on the decision-16 toxicity row when no margin was stated? The draft scores contra; the checker moved dose[6] only.
- model_first_use_year when the model paper postdates the trial start (ODM-204: trial start 2015-02, model paper 2018-02): open the strip at the registry start as proxy, or at the publication, and is a same-year untested opener required when model and trial papers share the year?
- Conference-only patient data with no DOI (ARMOR2 AR-V7 subset, EORTC-NCI-AACR 2014; Shore 2016 ASCO seviteronel phase 1/2): are they admissible at any tier, and if not, how is a prospective patient-level comparison that only exists there recorded?
- Imported safety harm when a same-year in-indication source exists (Bardia 2018 breast DLTs vs Gupta 2018 prostate: no DLT through 750 mg, dizziness 52%): which source sets the entry and does the import still need imported_from?
- Retrospective biomarker subset (galeterone-ctc-ar-variant-assay efficacy[8]): the Epic Sciences CTC AR C-terminal-loss assay was run on banked ARMOR2 samples and correlated with PSA responses already known (6/7). The draft opens the track untested; decision 10 makes a model run after the clinic knew the answer a retrodiction (retro), but here the subset analysis is itself the first patient read, so it reproduces no prior human result. Opener (untested) or retro?
- Assay change between hypothesis and test on one model_id: ARMOR2 used Epic Sciences CTC AR C-terminal-loss protein; ARMOR3-SV selected on the Johns Hopkins AdnaTest CTC AR-V7 mRNA assay. Are these one system (patient CTC AR-variant assays, as drafted) or two? If two, the ARMOR3-SV contra sits on a track with no opener and the Epic assay was never tested prospectively.
- comparison_stated when the comparing paper restates the model result from a cited paper: Maity 2016 quotes Rafferty 2014's 10-fold lyase-over-hydroxylase enzyme selectivity (its ref 8) and reads the patient's unchanged cortisol against it; the enzyme data are not Maity's own. Statement (as drafted, engagement[8] and safety[8]) or pairing with model_src Rafferty 2014 under rule 1? Related to question 3.

Missed comparisons:

- Tokai 10-K / press release 2016-07-26 (tier 5, SEC filing text at https://ir.eledon.com/static-files/0580574b-a213-4a9b-83f1-dab0fd0e9049): DMC recommendation 2016-07-25 that ARMOR3-SV would likely miss rPFS, no safety concerns cited. The draft cites it in judgement only; under rule 2 it is a dated tier-5 contra entry (trial_unreported true) three years before Taplin 2019.
- ARMOR2 AR-V7 subset (Taplin, EORTC-NCI-AACR 2014; news: https://ecancer.org/en/news/6615-galeterone-shows-activity-in-a-variant-form-of-castration-resistant-prostate-cancer): 6/7 AR-V7+ patients had PSA responses on galeterone, the patient-level prospective comparison on the CWR22Rv1 track that preceded ARMOR3-SV; conference-only, no DOI found (rule question 6).
- ClinicalTrials.gov NCT01709734 (ARMOR2) status TERMINATED, whyStopped: 'terminated prematurely ... upon recommendation from the DMC. Study was stopped due to lack of efficacy' - a registry datum bearing on the LAPC4-track class-level same; no results posted.
- Madan 2020 registry record: NCT02130700 (Innocrin, 17 enrolled, VT-464 BID and QD arms, 12-week PSA change primary, start 2014-04, no results) fits the paper; the draft's inferred NCT02445976 (197 enrolled, once-daily, post-enzalutamide or abiraterone) does not. Full text could not be opened (PMC bot check, Europe PMC 500) to confirm.
- Seviteronel class-level patient data outside the post-enzalutamide setting: NCT02012920 and NCT02361086 (phase 1/2 in CRPC, completed, no results posted) and the Shore 2016 ASCO abstract that Bardia 2018 cites as the source of the 750 mg phase 2 dose; not used by the draft.
- ODM-204 model dating: NCT02344017 started 2015-02, so the VCaP/monkey model existed by then; no public model source before Oksala (epub 2018-02-10) was found, so model_first_use_year 2018 is a publication date, not first use.
- Patient-derived organoids or ex-vivo cells compared with patients for galeterone or seviteronel: none found in one WebSearch; Alyamani 2017 (10.1016/j.chembiol.2017.05.020, resolves) is patient-serum galeterone metabolism explaining the miss, not a model prediction.
- ODM-204 cynomolgus-monkey / hCG-rat steroidogenesis and tolerability (Oksala 2019, doi 10.1016/j.jsbmb.2018.02.004, PMID 29438723; abstract confirmed: single oral 10-30 mg/kg dose-dependently inhibited adrenal and testicular steroid production in cynomolgus monkeys, similar in hCG-treated rats, 'well tolerated in both rodents and primates'). Checker (models) agrees with the sweep's split: engagement[6] (Peltola testosterone fall, model_src Oksala) and safety[5] rest on this system, not the VCaP xenograft, so under the one-system-one-model_id rule they belong on their own model_id (e.g. odm204-primate-steroidogenesis) with 2018 untested openers in engagement and safety, leaving odm204-vcap-xenograft with efficacy[5] and dose[4]. Not re-keyed here.
- Yin 2017 (doi 10.1124/dmd.117.075317, PMID 28373265; abstract confirmed): side-by-side CYP17A1 enzymatic analysis found seviteronel showed no significant lyase selectivity. A model-side contradiction of the seviteronel-cyp17a1-lyase-selectivity track's own claim with no patient comparison, so no entry; recorded so the track's 'same' is read against a disputed model.

## prostate-cancer-ezh2-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Membership vs register coverage: mevrometostat (PF-06821497, Pfizer; the class's only Phase 3 program, MEVPRO-1/2) is a selective EZH2 inhibitor absent from the reviewed class table (members lirametostat, tazemetostat, zeprumetostat). Decision 12 says only listed drugs score and its examples are sibling targets and non-selective drugs. Does a same-mechanism drug missing from the table score, or must the table be amended first? Four entries (engagement 3, dose 2, efficacy 3, safety 3) hinge on it.
- A registry record (tier 3) stating 'the primary endpoint was not met' while posting medians that favour the drug (CELLO-1 rPFS 22.1 vs 11.1 months, 95% CIs not overlapping) with no hazard ratio or test, in a trial terminated early: contra because the sponsor's own primary criterion failed (#64), or untested with source_calls_it unresolved because the predicted effect was not absent (#53, #90) and decision 10 says a non-significant trend in a terminated trial is not a patient result? The same answer decides ProSTAR (no sponsor verdict; ORR 7.9 vs 5.7%, composite 31.4 vs 17.1%).
- Decision 16 vs imported safety: the rat predicted T-LBL at >=2.8x the 800 mg BID AUC; MDS/AML/T-LBL appeared at the clinical exposure in epithelioid sarcoma and FL. Decision 16 would score a predicted harm with a misplaced margin in dose, but decision 12 lets only safety transfer across indications. Is the margin miss recorded anywhere when all of a drug's exposure data are imported?
- strengthens: true is defined for a second miss (#127); the draft uses it on three matched entries (engagement 2024, safety 2025, safety 2026). Should non-status-changing matched facts in engagement and safety, which have no patient_trials list, be folded into notes, or does strengthens extend to matches?
- May one paper serve as model_src on two tracks? Ku 2017's LNCaP/AR shRB1/shTP53 cells and GSK503 xenografts are used as the class-level xenograft track's model side while the same paper opens the patient-level GEMM track; the alternative is pairing the class track with Xiao 2018 or Bai 2019.
- A dose cell whose models stated no exposure or regimen target: the draft treats 'concentrations sufficient to inhibit H3K27 methylation' as the target and scores 'reached' on tumour H3K27me3 depletion. Is a PD-depletion criterion a dose target under decision 4, or is such a cell untested throughout (or empty)?

Missed comparisons:

- zeprumetostat (SHR2554, moiety 32177) is a listed member the draft never names (its note lists mevrometostat instead): mCRPC trial NCT03741712 (SHR2554 +/- SHR3680) terminated 2019-09 at n=9 with no results posted, so no admissible comparison, but the member list must match lists/class_audit/class_members.csv.
- Patient-derived organoid track: Puca et al. 2018 Nat Commun 9:2404 (doi 10.1038/s41467-018-04495-z) grew NEPC patient-derived organoids/PDX and tested EZH2 inhibition; an ex_vivo, patient-level model for the class in prostate with no patient comparison (untested track, model_kind ex_vivo).
- Missed tier-1 source for the CELLO-1 phase 2 result: ESMO 2024 abstract 1603P, Ann Oncol 35 suppl (doi 10.1016/j.annonc.2024.08.1684): 'Median rPFS by BCR was 16.6 and 13.8 months for TAZ+ENZ and ENZ, respectively' (fragment via search index; full text not retrievable). Re-dates the CELLO-1 entry to 2024 under decision 7.
- ProSTAR program decision, tier 5: Constellation 10-K FY2020 (sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1434418/000156459021007783/cnst-10k_20201231.htm): 'In June 2020, we announced that we plan to discontinue development of CPI-1205 after completion of ProSTAR' - a portfolio decision, not an efficacy verdict; belongs in patient_trials.

## prostate-cancer-folh1-directed-depleters

Rule questions:

- Dating (rule 7): does a trial's registry results posting date an entry whose comparison (here a PSMA-expression subgroup association) does not appear in the registry record, or only entries the record itself supports?
- Decision 16: is a qualitative pharmacokinetic statement in a biodistribution paper ('rapid clearance from the kidneys') a stated tolerability margin, or must it be a NOAEL/MTD or a tolerated dose at or above a stated effective one? And does a mouse activity margin (120 MBq effective without subacute haematotoxicity) count when MBq per mouse cannot be scaled to human GBq?
- Decision 4/16 coupling: when the model states only mouse mg/kg with no human-unit exposure target and the clinic was toxicity-capped at a lower dose intensity (PSMA ADC 2.5 mg/kg q3w vs 1-6 mg/kg weekly), is efficacy untested (never dosed to the model's regimen) or scored on the trial with dose untested for want of a target?
- Safety silence: when the model species expresses the target far less in the organ at risk (rodent salivary PSMA lowest, Roy 2020) and the human uptake is largely non-PSMA-mediated (Rupp 2019), is the harm 'measurable in the model species' (contra, as drafted) or outside what the species could show, like CRS in a mouse?
- Track membership: does a syngeneic murine line transduced with human PSMA in immunocompetent mice (RM1-hPSMA) share a model_id with human LNCaP / PC-3 PIP xenografts in nude mice, or is it a second system?
- Rule 7: does a patient-level same following a class-level same on the same track (engagement 3, efficacy 3) warrant its own entry with strengthens, or does it belong in patient_trials?
- Efficacy with three registry co-primary measures that split (PSA50 14%, CTC50 78%, radiologic SD): which one is 'the primary endpoint' when the model predicted tumour regression?
- ARROW: a phase 2 whose PSA50 primary was met while unpowered rPFS/OS were not improved and the sponsor took the drug no further stays same/patient_trials under #53 as drafted; confirm that a met primary in a combination trial with a negative outcome signal never scores contra.
- (models check) Decision 10 vs decision 4 'conditions the model never tested': when a retrodiction's own source reads the discordance as a mechanism the assay cannot measure (frozen-section autoradiography cannot show non-PSMA-mediated in-vivo gland accumulation, Rupp 2019), is the failed reproduction contra (literal decision 10, as drafted) or untested/outside scope, like CRS in a mouse (rule question 4 above)?
- (models check) #58 with a truncated regulator sentence: the FDA fragment's end sits on a page withheld from the published PDF; may the entry's quote be replaced by a second regulator's full statement (EMA EPAR EMA/871459/2022, 'little toxicity', NOAELs), or must it stay on the fragment at medium confidence?
- (models check) comparison_stated within one regulator document: do a nonclinical null (FDA's Assessment, 5.1) and a clinical attribution to radiation (Applicant's Position, 8.2.1) in the same review count as a stated comparison when neither section cites the other?
- (models check) Decision 16: the EPAR states ligand-mass NOAEL margins for the cold ligand (15x repeat-dose rat, 150x/400x single-dose rat/minipig vs 275 ug). Is a ligand-mass margin a 'stated margin' for the dose cell of a radioligand whose clinical ceiling is radiation absorbed dose (dose 3 adjudicated untested), or does it belong to the tox track only?

Missed comparisons:

- Cold-ligand rat and minipig toxicology (FDA review NDA 215833 nonclinical section: 'Single dose IV administration of 175Lu-PSMA-617 and unlabeled PSMA-617 caused no toxicologic [findings]'; clinical section: 'the main expected toxicities of 177Lu-PSMA-617 are related to radiation damage to normal tissues') is a stated null prediction (#58) that held in VISION; it should be its own safety track (same, 2022, tier 2, model_kind animal, comparison_stated true), not set aside as unquotable.
- Rupp 2019 (doi 10.2967/jnumed.118.222307, PMID 30737300): human submandibular-gland tissue autoradiography with 177Lu-PSMA-617 plus IHC against the same patients' 68Ga-PSMA-11 PET; an ex vivo patient-tissue model built after the salivary harm was known that failed to reproduce the in vivo uptake ('minimal signal on ARG' vs SUVmax 23.5), i.e. a retrodiction scored contra on its own track (decision 10, model_kind ex_vivo).
- Fendler 2017 syngeneic RM1-hPSMA model (immunocompetent C57Bl6, murine line transduced with human PSMA) is drawn on the LNCaP/PC-3 xenograft track; if it is a separate system it needs its own model_id, and its 'without subacute hematologic toxicity' at the effective 120 MBq is the class's only stated tolerability margin.
- Searched for LuCaP PDX or patient-derived organoid comparisons with radioligand patients (fc.py search, two queries): none found beyond DCFPyL imaging of PDX/organoids with no patient comparison; nothing to add on that front.

## prostate-cancer-parp1-2-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Dose when the model stated no exposure, occupancy or regimen target at all (BRCA2 xenografts dosed with AG14361/NU1025; AR-PARP xenografts): is 'reached and worked' scored same derived from efficacy, as drafted, or untested because there was no target to reach?
- A patient-level selection panel that is right for some genotypes (BRCA2, PALB2) and wrong for others (CDK12, mostly ATM): does the 'one drug's contra beats another's same' precedence extend to genotypes (contra, as drafted), or does the status table's 'partial agreement is same + source_calls_it partial' govern? The draft's contra + partial is a combination the table does not define.
- Is a pre-specified futility gate on a composite endpoint (MAGNITUDE Cohort 2; the cohort's rPFS primary was never tested) an endpoint_role poc_gate, or should the entry record the composite as the endpoint with a note?
- Does a tier-5 press release set the entry year under rule 6, which lists only registry posting, online publication, label and print? (MAGNITUDE Cohort 2 futility: Janssen 2022-02-15 vs Chi 2023; PROpel: AZ 2021-09-24 vs Clarke 2022.)
- Retrodiction under decision 10 ('built or run after the clinic already knew'): dated by the experiments, the manuscript receipt (Rafiei 2019-10-22, three weeks after ESMO PROfound), or publication (2020-03-03)? I scored retro because the paper's own introduction cites TOPARP-B's ATM 11% and PROfound; the drafter read the authors' framing as prospective.
- A trial selected on a broader gene panel than the model's biomarker (PROfound cohort A: BRCA2 about 52%, BRCA1/2 about 65%, ATM the rest): does the 80% rule for unselected trials apply by analogy, or does a gene-level exploratory subgroup (BRCA2 HR in the appendix or EMA EPAR) carry the BRCA2 track?
- Decision 12 safety imports of a xenograft efficacy study whose only tolerability readout was body weight (pamiparib, Xiong 2020): is its silence on cytopenias 'askable silence' when nobody relied on it for safety and the drug's toxicology species (NMPA dossier, not public) did predict marrow toxicity? If yes, every efficacy xenograft with a body-weight line becomes a contra safety track for the class.
- model_filed when the filing cites the model's companion paper (Farmer 2005) but not the entry's src (Bryant 2005): true as drafted, or false? Separately McCabe 2006 is not cited at all (McCabe appears only as a Farmer 2005 co-author), so the hrr-gene-panel entries' model_filed true looks like a grep false positive.
- models check: does a clinical-pharmacology DDI PBPK (olaparib exposure under CYP3A modulation, i.e. a dose-modification regimen) belong in the dose cell, which asks whether the model's exposure target was reached and sufficient? Admissible by the letter (#91 regimen prediction; in_silico model_kind) but it predicts neither the efficacious exposure nor its sufficiency, and admitting it makes every FDA-reviewed PBPK DDI model a dose track on every small-molecule tile.
- models check: decision 3 admits healthy-volunteer PK for dose; decision 12 lets cross-indication comparisons transfer only for safety with imported_from set. The olaparib DDI studies (D0816C00007/8) enrolled advanced-solid-tumour patients inside the ovarian NDA and are scored in a prostate dose cell with imported_from null, while safety index 1 from the same review sets MONDO:0008170. Which rule governs, and which MONDO (solid tumours or ovarian) would imported_from carry?
- models check: decision 10 when an in-silico model predicted prospectively (Simcyp V12 built while the DDI studies were ongoing; FDA IR 2014-03-05 asked for verification against their interim results) but the source reports only the re-fitted V13.1 model's agreement: retro as drafted, or same + partial on the unreported prospective prediction?

Missed comparisons:

- FDA ODAC 2023-04-28 on sNDA 208558/S-025 (PROpel): FDA briefing addendum https://www.fda.gov/media/167484/download and meeting page https://www.fda.gov/advisory-committees/advisory-committee-calendar/april-28-2023-meeting-oncologic-drugs-advisory-committee-meeting-announcement-04282023; committee voted 11-1 to restrict olaparib+abiraterone to BRCAm and FDA found the ITT benefit mainly attributable to the BRCAm subgroup. A tier-2 record that the AR-PARP all-comer prediction failed for olaparib too; the 2023 Lynparza label (BRCAm only) follows from it. Not cited anywhere in the draft.
- EMA Lynparza variation II/0036 EPAR (EMA/541236/2020; CHMP positive opinion Sept 2020; indication BRCA1/2-mutated mCRPC only, ATM and other HRR genes excluded on PROfound gene-level data): https://www.ema.europa.eu/en/documents/variation-report/lynparza-h-c-3726-ii-0036-epar-assessment-report-variation_en.pdf. Earliest tier-2 record of the HRR-panel miss (would date efficacy track hrr-gene-panel-cell-screen contra to 2020) and an ATM-specific datum for the ATM track. Downloaded; the pdftotext layout fragmented the gene-level sentences, so the quote still needs pulling.
- Beshiri 2018 Clin Cancer Res, LuCaP PDX-derived organoid platform (https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6125202): 'maximum cytotoxicity to the PARP inhibitor olaparib in BRCA2-/- organoids, similar to responses observed in patients'. A patient-derived model whose own source compares it with patients, after TOPARP-A, so a retro track at prediction_level patient; no track in the draft.
- Karkampouna 2021 Nat Commun, 'Patient-derived xenografts and organoids model therapy response in prostate cancer' (PMID 33602919): PDX/organoid response vs donor-patient response; check for a PARP-inhibitor arm before adding a track.
- Fuzuloparib (member drug) FUZUPRO phase 3 NCT04691804, fuzuloparib + AAP vs placebo + AAP first-line mCRPC with unselected and DRD cohorts; interim results ASCO 2026 abstract 5008 (meeting abstract, not an admissible tier); no registry results, press release or paper found. The draft never names the member or its (absent) model basis.
- Janssen press release 2022-02-15 (MAGNITUDE first results: HRR-negative cohort met prespecified futility, composite HR 1.09, 95% CI 0.75-1.59) and AstraZeneca press release 2021-09-24 (PROpel primary met): tier-5 records that precede Chi 2023 and Clarke 2022; they change year only if rule 6 admits press releases (rule question 4).
- Niraparib secondary pharmacology in the bundled Zejula 2017 review (DAT binding, blood-pressure and heart-rate rise in dogs) vs hypertension in MAGNITUDE: a safety same on the rat/dog track that the draft mentions in a confidence_note but does not score.

## psoriasis-anti-il17a-antibodies

Rule questions:

- Dose, decision 16: the 9-month cynomolgus NOAEL existed in the IND before Phase 3, but the only public statement of the margin is the 2015 FDA review, written after the trials. Does 'a model source stated the margin before the trial' mean the sponsor's filed data (model_filed: true) or a public source dated before the trial?
- Safety, IBD: Puel 2011 says IL-17A/F are 'otherwise largely redundant', which predicts no other harm, but the label warns of new or worse Crohn's disease and UC (0.1%/0.2% vs 0%). The rules don't say whether a small human genetic cohort 'could have shown' a harm this rare. If it could, the silence contradicts the label (contra); if not, it is no miss. The drafter took the second reading and did not score it.
- Efficacy, magnitude: when the model's claim is relative potency (izokibep 30-fold more effective per mg/kg than ixekizumab) and patients needed the same mg dose, should magnitude be scored on dose (off_by_>5x) rather than left direction_only on PASI 90?

Missed comparisons:

- No failure found for any member (ixekizumab, izokibep, netakimab, vunakizumab) in psoriasis. Positive patient trials not listed in patient_trials (they would change no status): vunakizumab Phase III (e.g. PMID 42952621, doi 10.3389/fimmu.2026.1894231) and netakimab Phase III PLANETA (citation not retrieved in budget). No press-release or SEC search was run for izokibep's later program; any failures I know of are outside psoriasis (not verified here).

## psoriasis-anti-il23a-antibodies

Rule questions:

- Engagement on a model whose only readouts are the disease histology: the xenograft measured acanthosis and T-cell infiltrate, which is also the efficacy readout, and its cytokine transcripts did not move. Scoring engagement same on the same histology as efficacy counts one result twice; should engagement be untested (or an empty list) when the model used no readout distinct from its efficacy endpoint?
- Decision 16 with no floor: when no model stated an efficacy exposure target (the xenograft used 60 mg/kg of a different antibody), can the dose track be same on a held NOAEL margin alone, or should margin_stated same require that a floor existed and was reached?
- Decision 16 'stated before the trial': the cyno NOAELs were the highest doses in studies run before phase 3, but the only public source stating the margin against the clinical dose is the 2018 EPAR written after approval. Does a sponsor tox report summarised post hoc by a regulator satisfy the margin_stated timing test, or does margin_stated need a pre-trial public source?
- A harm the species raised that patients cannot show: pregnant-cyno neonatal deaths at 6x and 59x MRHD (also in one control, 'clinical significance unknown') have no patient comparison because pregnancy is excluded from trials. The safety rules score a predicted harm that never appeared as contra/opposite; is an unverifiable predicted harm left unscored, or recorded untested on its own track?
- (models) Paired comparison dated when the model side was published after the patient side: the intradermal mouse assay was run pre-FIH (~2013) but public only in Steere 2023 / FDA 2023, the patient data in 2015. Decision 7 dates 'the comparison'; is the year 2023 (first date the pairing was possible, as drafted) or 2015 (patient result)? The cf-cftr convention assumes the model paper comes first.
- (models) Readout identity at the downstream tier: engagement asks whether 'the PD readout the model used' moved. Kopp Fig 2 shows the model's keratin-16 and epidermal thickness; Fig 6 shows a different IL-23-pathway gene panel, and IL-17A/IL-17F mRNA are not shown. Is a same-pathway, different-analyte panel enough for same, or must the analyte match (K16 same, IL-17 untested)?
- (models) A retrodiction whose source juxtaposes model rank with cross-trial PASI 90 (Zhou 2021 Table 1) but states the translation 'is not known and would require carefully controlled clinical studies': retro with source_calls_it unresolved (as drafted) or untested with unresolved under #90 ('tested, could not resolve')? The comparison also rests on a secondary, cross-trial endpoint, while the met primaries give the same retro reading.

Missed comparisons:

- Mirikizumab's own in-vivo pharmacodynamic models have no track: an acute systemic human IL-23 neutralisation assay in mice (IL-17 production) and an acute local intradermal human IL-23 skin assay (IL-17A, IL-17F and keratin-16 mRNA, epidermal thickening), both dosed with LY3074828 itself (product_in_model true). Never compared with patient PD, so an untested engagement track; source FDA Omvoh Multidiscipline Review BLA 761279 (https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/nda/2023/761279Orig1s000MultidisciplineR.pdf), nonclinical pharmacology summary and study report excerpts (bundle txt lines ~16040-16052, 16945-16959).
- IL23R R381Q loss-of-function carriers (human_genetic, an in-scope model_kind) are the class rationale Tonel 2010 itself cites ('disease association with single nucleotide polymorphisms in the IL23R gene') and were never put beside member-drug trial results; candidate untested track with Cargill 2007 Am J Hum Genet doi:10.1086/511051 (PMID 17236132) and Sarin 2011 PNAS doi:10.1073/pnas.1017854108 (PMID 21606346). Lower priority; list only if the atlas is tracking genetic models elsewhere.

## psoriasis-jak1-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- Cross-indication efficacy track: decision 8 opens a cell on the filed cross-indication model, the drafter brief says a class that went to patients on another indication's data leaves the cell empty, and decision 12 lets cross-indication evidence transfer for safety only. Can a cross_indication efficacy track ever score same/contra, is it untested by construction, or should the cell be blank?
- Decision 16 with no stated effective exposure: the monkey NOAEL margin held and patients were dosed to clinical effect, but no model source ever stated an effective exposure target. Does 'dosed to target' require a stated target, or does the clinically effective dose stand in for it?
- magnitude 'opposite' is defined (decision 5) for a predicted harm that never appeared. When the model predicted the absence of a harm (JAK2-axis sparing) and the harm appeared, is that 'opposite', 'direction_only', or null?
- Does an expectation sentence in a model paper's abstract ('a JAK1-selective inhibitor would be expected to ... avoid inhibition of the JAK2 homodimer') count as the model's stated safety prediction, given #111 sets aside mechanism-attribution sentences that name no species or study?

## psoriasis-pde4-inhibitors

Rule questions:

- 1. Efficacy contra on NCT01300052: a sponsor-declared unpowered Phase 2b (n=68, 2:1) whose primary is numerically positive (17.4% vs 13.6%) with CI -14.3 to +21.8, followed by an agreed SPA and a 'deferred' Phase 3. Does decision 13 make a non-significant primary a failed primary (contra), or is an unpowered trial an inadmissible comparison, leaving the track same with source_calls_it unresolved?
- 2. Dose for a topical with no systemic exposure or occupancy target: the mouse ear-edema model ranked ointment strength only. Should the dose cell mirror efficacy through derived_from (as drafted) or be an empty list because no model stated an exposure target?
- 3. Year taken from a tier-5 press release or 8-K (2010, 2011) while src, status and registry_date come from a tier-3 record posted 2017-03-07. Rule 6 lists registry posting, online publication, label or print; is a tier-5 document that is not the entry's src allowed to set year?
- 4. Engagement for the same topical drug and the same PD readout measured in a sibling skin disease (crisaborole lesional transcriptome in AD, Bissonnette 2019; LEO 29102 skin-explant cAMP): v3 lets only safety import across indications. Should engagement import with imported_from, or stay untested as drafted?
- 5. Is a psoriasis micro-plaque test (NCT00875277: 24 subjects, 4 weeks, TCS on test fields, six arms) a patient trial with a primary endpoint for efficacy (as drafted for LEO 29102), or a human_challenge-type model system that cannot score efficacy on its own?
- 6. Retrodiction flag when the model paper post-dates the trial but the work plausibly preceded it (DRM02 Tissue Barriers 2020 vs 2013-14 trials; Felding 2014 vs the 2009 plaque test): does retrodiction follow the publication date or the date the work was done when the abstract cannot tell? Status is contra either way here; the flag and year differ.
- 7. A tier-5 sponsor statement that pools several indications ('proof-of-concept clinical trials ... not more effective than vehicle', psoriasis not named) with a registry record showing the psoriasis trial completed without results: admissible as the psoriasis patient side at low confidence with trial_unreported (as drafted), or untested?
- 8. [models check] Decision 11's partial rule is written for same ('same on the pre-specified readout with source_calls_it: partial'). Does it extend to retro? Jardet 2020 reproduced the vehicle-controlled plaque-test win only in the prophylactic regimen (main text, Figure 4A); in the therapeutic regimen the placebo cream lowered IL-17A/IL-22/IFN-gamma 'comparable to the effect by the PDE4 inhibitor cream' (Figure S4). Drafted retro + partial; the alternative is unresolved.
